# 4A-14｜我看得見兇手頭上的最後一句台詞

*I Can Read the Killer's Last Line*

第四批・第4級（3000–4000詞級）｜超能力推理｜全25章雙語教學初稿。

主角：秦墨（刑警）；重要角色：沈嵐（法醫）。

**編輯註：**故事事件與結局為本次獨立設計，配詞融入與敘事中間段採規則化擴寫；這是完整章節的教學初稿，尚未經人工文學潤稿。

## CHAPTER 01｜The Words Above His Head｜頭頂那句話

### 正文與翻譯

**EN 01**

THE KILLER IS YOU. Qin Mo had worked seventeen murder investigations without seeing words float above a suspect's head. Now the sentence hung in the interrogation room like an accusation written in acid, bright enough that he could not look away. The man across the table was a theatre usher who had attended the morning assembly at the opera house. His hands shook, but the new evidence did not prove murder: there was a receipt for two coffees and a badge recording one late exit. Qin blinked. The sentence vanished. 'You think I'm lying,' the man said. Qin knew what a frightened person sounded like, and he knew how often fear had been mistaken for guilt. He turned off his recorder before asking the question that mattered. 'Who taught you those words?'

**繁中 01**

「兇手是你。」秦墨辦過十七起命案，從沒看過句子浮在嫌疑人頭上。現在那行字像用酸液寫在偵訊室的指控，亮得讓他移不開眼。桌子對面的男人是歌劇院引座員，早上參加過員工集會。他的手在發抖，但新證據還不能證明殺人：只有兩杯咖啡的收據，以及一筆逾時離場的證件紀錄。秦墨眨眼，文字消失了。「你以為我在說謊。」男人說。秦墨聽得出恐懼，也知道恐懼有多常被誤認為有罪。他在問出真正重要的問題前關掉錄音機：「是誰教你那句話的？」

**EN 02**

An officer arrived with a broom to sweep away the broken glass beneath the suspect's chair. Qin stopped him; the shards were evidence, not an inconvenience. He and Shen Lan could construct a timeline from the theatre's entrance logs, but the last twenty minutes were missing. The usher had no reason to cooperate after Qin stared at him as though an invisible accusation were proof. Qin tried again, this time offering the man a chance to explain the missing period without being told what answer the police expected. 'We rehearsed a line,' the usher whispered. 'The coach asked me to look straight at the person across the table and say it.' The sentence returned above his head, exactly the same, and Qin finally understood the danger of seeing it.

**繁中 02**

一名警員拿著掃帚進來，要掃掉嫌疑人椅子下的碎玻璃。秦墨阻止他：碎片是證據，不是礙事的垃圾。他和沈嵐可以從劇院進出紀錄建構時間線，但最後二十分鐘不見了。秦墨先前用看著有罪者的眼神盯著引座員，對方沒有理由合作。他重新問話，這次不預設警方想聽的答案。「我們排練一句台詞。」引座員低聲說，「教練叫我看著桌子對面的人，直接說出來。」同一句話再次浮在男人頭上。秦墨終於明白看見它有多危險。

**EN 03**

A copper stage ticket lay on the counter with a hole punched in the wrong corner. The usher said it belonged to the dead speech coach, who always entered through the side passage. Qin asked whether the coach had been frightened. 'He called me a coward because I wouldn't repeat one sentence,' the usher replied. Qin looked for the words above the man's head. Nothing appeared. His ability seemed to have its own timing, and the absence of a vision offered no comfort. Shen Lan packed the ticket for fingerprint comparison, then pointed to the ticket machine's mechanical clock. Its time was four minutes behind the building's digital system. That difference placed the coach near the side door when the police thought he had already left.

**繁中 03**

櫃臺上有一張銅色舞台票，打孔位置錯了一角。引座員說票屬於死去的語言教練，對方總從側門出入。秦墨問教練是否害怕過。「他罵我是膽小鬼，因為我不肯重複一句話。」引座員回答。秦墨再看男人頭上，卻什麼字都沒有。這能力似乎有自己的時機，沒有異象並不代表安全。沈嵐把票裝袋準備比對指紋，又指向售票機的機械鐘：它比建築物的數位系統慢了四分鐘。這個差異顯示，警方原以為教練已離開時，他其實仍在側門附近。

**EN 04**

The theatre's director, whose job was to entertain the public, insisted the rehearsal was ordinary work. Qin asked why the suspect's facial expression had changed each time the accusation was repeated. A rehearsal recording answered part of the question: the speech coach had trained him to pause, lower his eyes, and look suddenly at the listener. The director tried to interfere with the playback, saying the recording was proprietary. Shen Lan requested the original file under the proper warrant and kept the director's objection in the report. 'You can own a performance,' she told him. 'You cannot own the physical facts of a murder.' Qin noticed that the sentence above the usher appeared only when the director stepped close enough to be heard.

**繁中 04**

劇院娛樂部主管堅稱排練只是普通工作。秦墨問為何引座員每次重複指控時，臉部表情都會改變。排練錄音解答了一部分：語言教練教他停頓、垂眼，再突然望向聽眾。主管企圖干擾播放，說錄音屬於商業資產。沈嵐依合法程序申請原檔，並把他的異議記進報告。「表演可以屬於你，」她說，「謀殺的客觀事實不能。」秦墨注意到，主管走到足夠靠近的位置時，引座員頭上的句子才會出現。

**EN 05**

A ministry official phoned during the search, nervous that the opera house might close before a visiting delegation arrived. The official's argument sounded persuasive: thousands of tickets had been sold, and the city could not afford another scandal. Qin said a closed room could be reopened after inspection, whereas contaminated evidence could not be made innocent again. Shen Lan took a photograph of the floor before the technicians moved the lights. In the reflection, a figure seemed to stand behind the speech coach's chair. Nobody had noticed that figure in the original photograph because the camera was angled toward the stage. Qin asked the officer to preserve every version rather than choose the most dramatic one.

**繁中 05**

搜索期間，部會官員打電話來，擔心歌劇院在外賓抵達前被關閉。他的論點聽來很有說服力：數千張票已賣出，城市受不了另一場醜聞。秦墨回答，封閉房間檢查後可以重開，遭污染的證據卻無法重新變乾淨。技師移動燈具前，沈嵐先拍下地板。反光裡，語言教練的椅子後似乎站著一道人影。原照片把鏡頭朝向舞台，所以誰也沒注意到。秦墨要求保留所有版本，而非只挑最戲劇化的一張。

**EN 06**

The phenomenon repeated just once more. As the usher walked toward the holding room, he turned by the service route and looked directly at Qin. THE KILLER IS YOU returned above his head, bright as before. But an employee waiting near the staircase said the coach had taught the usher that line several weeks earlier, long before the killings. A shady acting school had paid for the rehearsals, promising students that a convincing confession would secure them roles in a crime drama. Qin wrote down the school's address without assuming it explained the murder. He had one real piece of evidence now: somebody had prepared a sentence designed to make him stop investigating and start defending himself.

**繁中 06**

這種現象又出現了一次。引座員沿服務動線走向留置室時，轉身直望秦墨，頭上再次浮出「兇手是你」，與先前一樣明亮。但樓梯旁的員工說，教練好幾週前就教過他這句台詞，遠在命案之前。一家可疑的演員訓練所支付了排練費，承諾學生只要認罪演得夠真就能接到犯罪劇角色。秦墨記下學校地址，卻不假定它就能解釋謀殺。現在他至少有一項真正證據：有人事先準備了這句話，要讓他停止調查，轉而替自己辯護。

**EN 07**

The city police liked the slogan TRUST YOUR INSTINCTS. Qin had lived with that youthful confidence until one wrong confession cost a stranger six years. He now wrote down the precise usage of his impossible new ability: the words appeared before a person spoke, changed when the person expected a different question, and disappeared before anyone else could see them. Such observations could guide a search but could not replace a witness. Shen Lan arrived with the autopsy folder and found him staring at the blank wall. 'There are no words up there,' she said. 'There were,' he replied. She did not laugh. She opened the file to a photograph of the dead speech coach and pointed to a note beside his hand: FINAL LINE, REHEARSE TWICE.

**繁中 07**

市警局喜歡的口號是「相信直覺」。秦墨年輕時也曾充滿這種信心，直到一次錯誤供詞讓陌生人失去六年。他此刻寫下怪異能力的確切用法：文字在對方開口前出現，對方預期問題改變時也會變化，並且在其他人看見前消失。這些觀察可以指引搜尋，卻不能取代證人。沈嵐帶著驗屍卷宗進來，發現他盯著空牆。「牆上什麼都沒有。」她說。「剛才有。」他答。她沒有笑，而是翻到死去語言教練的照片，指著死者手邊的便條：「最後一句，排練兩次。」

### 理解測驗

**Q1. Which development is established in Chapter 1?**

A. The investigators decide that every earlier warning was fictional.

B. Detective Qin Mo saw a sentence hovering above a murder suspect before the man spoke: THE KILLER IS YOU. The words vanished when the suspect blinked.

C. The central problem disappears without further action.

**答案：B。** 刑警秦墨在嫌疑人開口前看見他頭上的一句話：兇手是你。對方眨眼後字就消失了。

**Q2. What new complication or decision changes the investigation in this chapter?**

A. All the witnesses agree to destroy their records.

B. The team abandons the people affected and declares a perfect victory.

C. Medical examiner Shen Lan warned Qin that a prediction could reflect fear, rehearsal, or manipulation rather than guilt.

**答案：C。** 法醫沈嵐提醒秦墨，預言可能源於恐懼、排練或操控，而不是罪證。


## CHAPTER 02｜The Victim's Last Appointment｜死者最後的約會

### 正文與翻譯

**EN 01**

The victim, a speech coach, had booked private sessions with three suspects who all rehearsed difficult confessions before the murder. Something had changed, but not everybody noticed it in the same order. Qin Mo listened to the account of the nearest worker, then to someone standing farther away. Their differences were not necessarily lies. Each person had been given a different view and a different reason to be afraid of admitting uncertainty. The station corridor sounded ordinary after midnight. Qin Mo found that the hardest part of investigating a powerful claim was giving an ordinary doubt enough time. A note about the autograph lay beside the old register, with a date that Qin Mo could not verify. On the next form, the action field read carve, but the witness had never approved the operation.

**繁中 01**

死者是語言教練，遇害前替三名嫌疑人安排私下課程，三人都練習過艱難的坦白。某件事改變了，但人們發現它的順序不同。秦墨先聽近處工作人員的描述，再聽遠處旁觀者說法。差異不一定是謊言：每個人看到的角度不同，承認不確定時所要承擔的風險也不同。午夜後警局走廊聲音平凡。秦墨發現調查強大宣稱時最難的是給平凡疑問足夠時間。一張談到「親筆簽名」的便條放在舊登記簿旁，日期讓秦墨無法核實。下一張表單把動作寫成「刻出」，見證人卻從未同意這項操作。

**EN 02**

Medical examiner Shen Lan warned Qin that a prediction could reflect fear, rehearsal, or manipulation rather than guilt. Qin Mo refused to pretend the previous decision had solved everything. The practical result was still there, useful but narrow. For each thing the team knew, there were two things it had not measured. Qin Mo drew a line through the claims that depended on speculation and asked Shen Lan to keep the original record intact. People disagreed about convenience; Qin Mo separated the feeling from the facts they could test. The report claimed cruelty, but Qin Mo wanted an example instead of a reassuring noun. Shen Lan noticed the word disguise in a note that someone had added after the first inspection.

**繁中 02**

法醫沈嵐提醒秦墨，預言可能源於恐懼、排練或操控，而不是罪證。秦墨不願假裝前一次決定已解決所有問題。成果確實存在，卻有明確界限。每個已知事項旁都還有未測量的部分。秦墨劃掉靠猜測成立的說法，請沈嵐保留原始紀錄。大家對「便利」意見不同，秦墨把感受與可檢驗的事實分開。報告聲稱有「殘忍」，秦墨要求具體例子，而不是安撫人的名詞。沈嵐注意到初次檢查後有人在筆記裡加上「偽裝」一詞。

**EN 03**

A small disagreement changed the schedule. Qin Mo wanted to proceed before evidence disappeared; Shen Lan wanted another check because a wrong conclusion might hurt people who could not easily defend themselves. They did not settle the matter by choosing a leader. They wrote down what a short, reversible test could actually establish. Shen Lan noticed the word elastic in a note that someone had added after the first inspection. A handwritten label read explosive, but no one could say who had written it there. The report claimed feedback, but Qin Mo wanted an example instead of a reassuring noun.

**繁中 03**

一場小爭執改變了時程。秦墨想趁證據消失前行動；沈嵐要求再核對一次，因為錯誤結論可能傷害難以自辯的人。他們沒有靠選領袖解決爭議，而是列出短暫、可逆測試真正能證明什麼。沈嵐注意到初次檢查後有人在筆記裡加上「有彈性的」一詞。手寫標籤寫著「爆炸性的」，卻沒有人知道是誰留下的。報告聲稱有「回饋」，秦墨要求具體例子，而不是安撫人的名詞。

**EN 04**

Qin realized the floating line might be words someone intended to say, not words guaranteed to be spoken. The plan had been amended three times, and each change left a trace. Qin Mo valued those corrections more than a perfect-looking first draft. If the outcome proved disappointing, the group would still be able to identify which assumption needed repair. Shen Lan watched for the quiet participant who had not yet been invited to speak. The term immigrant appeared in the revised file; Qin Mo checked its meaning against the original page. Shen Lan noticed the word initial in a note that someone had added after the first inspection. A handwritten label read mischief, but no one could say who had written it there.

**繁中 04**

秦墨察覺飄浮字句也許是對方想說的話，而非必然會說出口的句子。計畫改了三次，每次修改都留下痕跡。秦墨認為這些修正比看似完美的初稿更有價值。就算結果不理想，團隊仍能知道該修正哪一項假設。沈嵐也注意到那名一直沒有發言機會的人。修訂檔案出現「移民」一詞，秦墨拿原始頁面核對它的意思。沈嵐注意到初次檢查後有人在筆記裡加上「最初的」一詞。手寫標籤寫著「惡作劇」，卻沒有人知道是誰留下的。

**EN 05**

An official version reached the room before the practical work was finished. Qin Mo read it once, then compared it with what they had actually measured. The difference was not always malicious, but it could still hurt somebody. Shen Lan asked that the correction be written in language the affected people could understand. The station corridor sounded ordinary after midnight. Qin Mo found that the hardest part of investigating a powerful claim was giving an ordinary doubt enough time. The earlier photograph clearly showed a paw; the newer version did not, so Qin Mo kept both. The report claimed prevention, but Qin Mo wanted an example instead of a reassuring noun. Shen Lan wrote privilege in the margin and asked how the team would recognize it in practice.

**繁中 05**

實際工作還沒完成，官方版本就傳到現場。秦墨讀了一次，再比對真正測得的結果。差異未必都是惡意，卻仍可能傷害人。沈嵐要求把更正寫成受影響者看得懂的語言。午夜後警局走廊聲音平凡。秦墨發現調查強大宣稱時最難的是給平凡疑問足夠時間。較早的照片清楚顯示一項「爪子」，新版卻沒有，秦墨因此保留兩張。報告聲稱有「預防」，秦墨要求具體例子，而不是安撫人的名詞。沈嵐在頁邊寫下「特權」，追問團隊實際上如何辨認。

**EN 06**

A witness asked whether the team was taking sides. Qin Mo answered that refusing to invent a fact was not the same as refusing to care. What mattered was whether the procedure protected someone whose story was less convenient to tell. Shen Lan listened without interrupting and wrote down the question exactly as it had been asked. One witness used the word prominent; Shen Lan noted that a description was not a measurement. Shen Lan repeated the instruction to prosper, then asked whether anyone had the right to refuse. The term remark appeared in the revised file; Qin Mo checked its meaning against the original page.

**繁中 06**

一名見證人問團隊是否偏袒。秦墨回答，不捏造事實不代表冷漠。真正重要的是程序能否保護那個故事不方便被說出口的人。沈嵐沒有打斷，逐字記下原問題。一位證人用了「醒目的」一詞，沈嵐指出形容並不等於測量。沈嵐重述「繁榮」的指示，再問當事人是否有權拒絕。修訂檔案出現「評論；言語」一詞，秦墨拿原始頁面核對它的意思。

**EN 07**

Even after the group agreed on the immediate step, another question remained. The answer would not arrive through a dramatic announcement. Someone would have to return to the place, compare the earlier notes, and accept the possibility that the next piece of evidence might spoil an attractive theory. Qin Mo prepared to do exactly that. The interview recorder captured words but not the years of rehearsal behind them. Shen Lan insisted on laboratory tests before a dramatic confession became a charge. The term ruin appeared in the revised file; Qin Mo checked its meaning against the original page. On the next form, the action field read shift, but the witness had never approved the operation. A handwritten label read timetable, but no one could say who had written it there.

**繁中 07**

即使大家同意眼前步驟，另一個問題仍在。答案不會靠戲劇性公告降臨；有人得返回現場、比對舊筆記，並接受新證據可能推翻漂亮理論。秦墨準備做的正是這件事。偵訊錄音留下話語，留不下背後多年的排練。沈嵐堅持在戲劇性認罪變成指控前先完成實驗室檢驗。修訂檔案出現「毀壞」一詞，秦墨拿原始頁面核對它的意思。下一張表單把動作寫成「輪班」，見證人卻從未同意這項操作。手寫標籤寫著「時刻表」，卻沒有人知道是誰留下的。

|---|---|---|---|---|---|

### 理解測驗

**Q1. Which development is established in Chapter 2?**

A. The investigators decide that every earlier warning was fictional.

B. The victim, a speech coach, had booked private sessions with three suspects who all rehearsed difficult confessions before the murder.

C. The central problem disappears without further action.

**答案：B。** 死者是語言教練，遇害前替三名嫌疑人安排私下課程，三人都練習過艱難的坦白。

**Q2. What new complication or decision changes the investigation in this chapter?**

A. All the witnesses agree to destroy their records.

B. The team abandons the people affected and declares a perfect victory.

C. Qin realized the floating line might be words someone intended to say, not words guaranteed to be spoken.

**答案：C。** 秦墨察覺飄浮字句也許是對方想說的話，而非必然會說出口的句子。


## CHAPTER 03｜Two Lines, One Person｜同一人的兩句話

### 正文與翻譯

**EN 01**

A suspect's visible sentence changed after Qin showed her a photograph. The second line contradicted the first, although the woman had not lied aloud. Something had changed, but not everybody noticed it in the same order. Qin Mo listened to the account of the nearest worker, then to someone standing farther away. Their differences were not necessarily lies. Each person had been given a different view and a different reason to be afraid of admitting uncertainty. The interview recorder captured words but not the years of rehearsal behind them. Shen Lan insisted on laboratory tests before a dramatic confession became a charge. An official memo described the plan as accidental, but Qin Mo refused to treat an adjective as evidence. A note about the barrier lay beside the old register, with a date that Qin Mo could not verify.

**繁中 01**

秦墨出示照片後，嫌疑人頭上的句子改變了，第二句與第一句矛盾，女子卻未開口說謊。某件事改變了，但人們發現它的順序不同。秦墨先聽近處工作人員的描述，再聽遠處旁觀者說法。差異不一定是謊言：每個人看到的角度不同，承認不確定時所要承擔的風險也不同。偵訊錄音留下話語，留不下背後多年的排練。沈嵐堅持在戲劇性認罪變成指控前先完成實驗室檢驗。官方備忘錄形容計畫「意外的」，秦墨不願把形容詞當作證據。一張談到「柵欄」的便條放在舊登記簿旁，日期讓秦墨無法核實。

**EN 02**

Qin realized the floating line might be words someone intended to say, not words guaranteed to be spoken. Qin Mo refused to pretend the previous decision had solved everything. The practical result was still there, useful but narrow. For each thing the team knew, there were two things it had not measured. Qin Mo drew a line through the claims that depended on speculation and asked Shen Lan to keep the original record intact. On the next form, the action field read blink, but the witness had never approved the operation. An official memo described the plan as brutal, but Qin Mo refused to treat an adjective as evidence. The instructions used the verb choke without naming who would carry the risk; Qin Mo circled that gap.

**繁中 02**

秦墨察覺飄浮字句也許是對方想說的話，而非必然會說出口的句子。秦墨不願假裝前一次決定已解決所有問題。成果確實存在，卻有明確界限。每個已知事項旁都還有未測量的部分。秦墨劃掉靠猜測成立的說法，請沈嵐保留原始紀錄。下一張表單把動作寫成「眨眼」，見證人卻從未同意這項操作。官方備忘錄形容計畫「粗獷而堅固的」，秦墨不願把形容詞當作證據。指示裡用了「阻斷」這個動詞，卻沒說誰承擔風險；秦墨圈出這項缺漏。

**EN 03**

A small disagreement changed the schedule. Qin Mo wanted to proceed before evidence disappeared; Shen Lan wanted another check because a wrong conclusion might hurt people who could not easily defend themselves. They did not settle the matter by choosing a leader. They wrote down what a short, reversible test could actually establish. The term composition appeared in the revised file; Qin Mo checked its meaning against the original page. On the next form, the action field read evaluate, but the witness had never approved the operation. People disagreed about gratitude; Qin Mo separated the feeling from the facts they could test.

**繁中 03**

一場小爭執改變了時程。秦墨想趁證據消失前行動；沈嵐要求再核對一次，因為錯誤結論可能傷害難以自辯的人。他們沒有靠選領袖解決爭議，而是列出短暫、可逆測試真正能證明什麼。修訂檔案出現「結構；組成」一詞，秦墨拿原始頁面核對它的意思。下一張表單把動作寫成「評估」，見證人卻從未同意這項操作。大家對「感激」意見不同，秦墨把感受與可檢驗的事實分開。

**EN 04**

Shen Lan proposed blinded questioning so Qin's gift would not shape every interview and contaminate the evidence. The plan had been amended three times, and each change left a trace. Qin Mo valued those corrections more than a perfect-looking first draft. If the outcome proved disappointing, the group would still be able to identify which assumption needed repair. Shen Lan watched for the quiet participant who had not yet been invited to speak. A handwritten label read grave, but no one could say who had written it there. The report claimed intelligence, but Qin Mo wanted an example instead of a reassuring noun. One witness used the word mild; Shen Lan noted that a description was not a measurement.

**繁中 04**

沈嵐建議採盲測問話，以免秦墨的能力污染整個調查。計畫改了三次，每次修改都留下痕跡。秦墨認為這些修正比看似完美的初稿更有價值。就算結果不理想，團隊仍能知道該修正哪一項假設。沈嵐也注意到那名一直沒有發言機會的人。手寫標籤寫著「嚴重的」，卻沒有人知道是誰留下的。報告聲稱有「情報」，秦墨要求具體例子，而不是安撫人的名詞。一位證人用了「溫和的」一詞，沈嵐指出形容並不等於測量。

**EN 05**

An official version reached the room before the practical work was finished. Qin Mo read it once, then compared it with what they had actually measured. The difference was not always malicious, but it could still hurt somebody. Shen Lan asked that the correction be written in language the affected people could understand. The interview recorder captured words but not the years of rehearsal behind them. Shen Lan insisted on laboratory tests before a dramatic confession became a charge. One witness used the word mountainous; Shen Lan noted that a description was not a measurement. People disagreed about neglect; Qin Mo separated the feeling from the facts they could test. The instructions used the verb offend without naming who would carry the risk; Qin Mo circled that gap.

**繁中 05**

實際工作還沒完成，官方版本就傳到現場。秦墨讀了一次，再比對真正測得的結果。差異未必都是惡意，卻仍可能傷害人。沈嵐要求把更正寫成受影響者看得懂的語言。偵訊錄音留下話語，留不下背後多年的排練。沈嵐堅持在戲劇性認罪變成指控前先完成實驗室檢驗。一位證人用了「山區的」一詞，沈嵐指出形容並不等於測量。大家對「疏忽」意見不同，秦墨把感受與可檢驗的事實分開。指示裡用了「冒犯」這個動詞，卻沒說誰承擔風險；秦墨圈出這項缺漏。

**EN 06**

A witness asked whether the team was taking sides. Qin Mo answered that refusing to invent a fact was not the same as refusing to care. What mattered was whether the procedure protected someone whose story was less convenient to tell. Shen Lan listened without interrupting and wrote down the question exactly as it had been asked. The term philosopher appeared in the revised file; Qin Mo checked its meaning against the original page. Shen Lan wrote reform in the margin and asked how the team would recognize it in practice. People disagreed about revenge; Qin Mo separated the feeling from the facts they could test.

**繁中 06**

一名見證人問團隊是否偏袒。秦墨回答，不捏造事實不代表冷漠。真正重要的是程序能否保護那個故事不方便被說出口的人。沈嵐沒有打斷，逐字記下原問題。修訂檔案出現「哲學家」一詞，秦墨拿原始頁面核對它的意思。沈嵐在頁邊寫下「改革」，追問團隊實際上如何辨認。大家對「報復」意見不同，秦墨把感受與可檢驗的事實分開。

**EN 07**

Even after the group agreed on the immediate step, another question remained. The answer would not arrive through a dramatic announcement. Someone would have to return to the place, compare the earlier notes, and accept the possibility that the next piece of evidence might spoil an attractive theory. Qin Mo prepared to do exactly that. The station corridor sounded ordinary after midnight. Qin Mo found that the hardest part of investigating a powerful claim was giving an ordinary doubt enough time. A handwritten label read tendency, but no one could say who had written it there. A note about the twig lay beside the old register, with a date that Qin Mo could not verify. Shen Lan noticed the word urge in a note that someone had added after the first inspection.

**繁中 07**

即使大家同意眼前步驟，另一個問題仍在。答案不會靠戲劇性公告降臨；有人得返回現場、比對舊筆記，並接受新證據可能推翻漂亮理論。秦墨準備做的正是這件事。午夜後警局走廊聲音平凡。秦墨發現調查強大宣稱時最難的是給平凡疑問足夠時間。手寫標籤寫著「傾向」，卻沒有人知道是誰留下的。一張談到「樹枝」的便條放在舊登記簿旁，日期讓秦墨無法核實。沈嵐注意到初次檢查後有人在筆記裡加上「催促」一詞。

實際工作還沒完成，官方版本就傳到現場。秦墨讀了一次，再比對真正測得的結果。差異未必都是惡意，卻仍可能傷害人。沈嵐要求把更正寫成受影響者看得懂的語言。午夜後警局走廊聲音平凡。秦墨發現調查強大宣稱時最難的是給平凡疑問足夠時間。修訂檔案出現「殺人犯」一詞，秦墨拿原始頁面核對它的意思。沈嵐注意到初次檢查後有人在筆記裡加上「荒謬的事」一詞。較早的照片清楚顯示一項「運行軌道」，新版卻沒有，秦墨因此保留兩張。

**EN 06**

A witness asked whether the team was taking sides. Qin Mo answered that refusing to invent a fact was not the same as refusing to care. What mattered was whether the procedure protected someone whose story was less convenient to tell. Shen Lan listened without interrupting and wrote down the question exactly as it had been asked. An official memo described the plan as passive, but Qin Mo refused to treat an adjective as evidence. A note about the possession lay beside the old register, with a date that Qin Mo could not verify. Shen Lan pointed to the researcher in the inventory and asked why its location had changed.

**繁中 06**

一名見證人問團隊是否偏袒。秦墨回答，不捏造事實不代表冷漠。真正重要的是程序能否保護那個故事不方便被說出口的人。沈嵐沒有打斷，逐字記下原問題。官方備忘錄形容計畫「被動的」，秦墨不願把形容詞當作證據。一張談到「擁有權」的便條放在舊登記簿旁，日期讓秦墨無法核實。沈嵐指向清單上的「研究員」，追問其位置為何改變。

**EN 07**

Even after the group agreed on the immediate step, another question remained. The answer would not arrive through a dramatic announcement. Someone would have to return to the place, compare the earlier notes, and accept the possibility that the next piece of evidence might spoil an attractive theory. Qin Mo prepared to do exactly that. The interview recorder captured words but not the years of rehearsal behind them. Shen Lan insisted on laboratory tests before a dramatic confession became a charge. Shen Lan noticed the word rhyme in a note that someone had added after the first inspection. A handwritten label read translator, but no one could say who had written it there. The summary called the situation urgent; Qin Mo asked which observation justified that description.

**繁中 07**

即使大家同意眼前步驟，另一個問題仍在。答案不會靠戲劇性公告降臨；有人得返回現場、比對舊筆記，並接受新證據可能推翻漂亮理論。秦墨準備做的正是這件事。偵訊錄音留下話語，留不下背後多年的排練。沈嵐堅持在戲劇性認罪變成指控前先完成實驗室檢驗。沈嵐注意到初次檢查後有人在筆記裡加上「童謠」一詞。手寫標籤寫著「翻譯員」，卻沒有人知道是誰留下的。摘要以「緊急的」形容情況，秦墨追問這種說法有何觀察根據。

### 理解測驗

**Q1. Which development is established in Chapter 4?**

A. The investigators decide that every earlier warning was fictional.

B. Qin wrote questions on cards and let another officer conduct the interview without telling him which suspect had appeared in the first vision.

C. The central problem disappears without further action.

**答案：B。** 秦墨先寫下問題卡，再讓另一名警員主持問話，沒有透露首次異象來自誰。

**Q2. What new complication or decision changes the investigation in this chapter?**

A. All the witnesses agree to destroy their records.

B. The team abandons the people affected and declares a perfect victory.

C. The recorded answers disagreed with the visions in predictable ways; the sentences proved sensitive to anticipation rather than truth.

**答案：C。** 錄下來的回答以可預測方式偏離異象，證明文字會受預期影響，而非直接表示真相。


## CHAPTER 05｜The Room With No Echo｜沒有回音的房間

### 正文與翻譯

**EN 01**

A hidden studio contained soundproof panels and recordings of the murder victim coaching people to make confessions they did not believe. Something had changed, but not everybody noticed it in the same order. Qin Mo listened to the account of the nearest worker, then to someone standing farther away. Their differences were not necessarily lies. Each person had been given a different view and a different reason to be afraid of admitting uncertainty. The interview recorder captured words but not the years of rehearsal behind them. Shen Lan insisted on laboratory tests before a dramatic confession became a charge. The instructions used the verb adopt without naming who would carry the risk; Qin Mo circled that gap. Shen Lan noticed the word agency in a note that someone had added after the first inspection.

**繁中 01**

密室裡有隔音板，以及死者教人練習自己並不相信的供詞的錄音。某件事改變了，但人們發現它的順序不同。秦墨先聽近處工作人員的描述，再聽遠處旁觀者說法。差異不一定是謊言：每個人看到的角度不同，承認不確定時所要承擔的風險也不同。偵訊錄音留下話語，留不下背後多年的排練。沈嵐堅持在戲劇性認罪變成指控前先完成實驗室檢驗。指示裡用了「採用」這個動詞，卻沒說誰承擔風險；秦墨圈出這項缺漏。沈嵐注意到初次檢查後有人在筆記裡加上「機構」一詞。

**EN 02**

The recorded answers disagreed with the visions in predictable ways; the sentences proved sensitive to anticipation rather than truth. Qin Mo refused to pretend the previous decision had solved everything. The practical result was still there, useful but narrow. For each thing the team knew, there were two things it had not measured. Qin Mo drew a line through the claims that depended on speculation and asked Shen Lan to keep the original record intact. People disagreed about behavior; Qin Mo separated the feeling from the facts they could test. The instructions used the verb cherish without naming who would carry the risk; Qin Mo circled that gap. Shen Lan noticed the word congress in a note that someone had added after the first inspection.

**繁中 02**

錄下來的回答以可預測方式偏離異象，證明文字會受預期影響，而非直接表示真相。秦墨不願假裝前一次決定已解決所有問題。成果確實存在，卻有明確界限。每個已知事項旁都還有未測量的部分。秦墨劃掉靠猜測成立的說法，請沈嵐保留原始紀錄。大家對「行為」意見不同，秦墨把感受與可檢驗的事實分開。指示裡用了「珍惜」這個動詞，卻沒說誰承擔風險；秦墨圈出這項缺漏。沈嵐注意到初次檢查後有人在筆記裡加上「議會」一詞。

**EN 03**

A small disagreement changed the schedule. Qin Mo wanted to proceed before evidence disappeared; Shen Lan wanted another check because a wrong conclusion might hurt people who could not easily defend themselves. They did not settle the matter by choosing a leader. They wrote down what a short, reversible test could actually establish. Shen Lan noticed the word conscience in a note that someone had added after the first inspection. A handwritten label read decoration, but no one could say who had written it there. The term discourage appeared in the revised file; Qin Mo checked its meaning against the original page.

**繁中 03**

一場小爭執改變了時程。秦墨想趁證據消失前行動；沈嵐要求再核對一次，因為錯誤結論可能傷害難以自辯的人。他們沒有靠選領袖解決爭議，而是列出短暫、可逆測試真正能證明什麼。沈嵐注意到初次檢查後有人在筆記裡加上「良心」一詞。手寫標籤寫著「裝飾」，卻沒有人知道是誰留下的。修訂檔案出現「使卻步」一詞，秦墨拿原始頁面核對它的意思。

**EN 04**

The recordings included Qin's voice from years earlier, reopening a case he thought he had settled. The plan had been amended three times, and each change left a trace. Qin Mo valued those corrections more than a perfect-looking first draft. If the outcome proved disappointing, the group would still be able to identify which assumption needed repair. Shen Lan watched for the quiet participant who had not yet been invited to speak. The term fundamental appeared in the revised file; Qin Mo checked its meaning against the original page. On the next form, the action field read horrify, but the witness had never approved the operation. An official memo described the plan as influential, but Qin Mo refused to treat an adjective as evidence.

**繁中 04**

錄音裡也有秦墨多年前的聲音，讓一樁他以為已結案的案件重新浮現。計畫改了三次，每次修改都留下痕跡。秦墨認為這些修正比看似完美的初稿更有價值。就算結果不理想，團隊仍能知道該修正哪一項假設。沈嵐也注意到那名一直沒有發言機會的人。修訂檔案出現「根本的；基本的」一詞，秦墨拿原始頁面核對它的意思。下一張表單把動作寫成「使驚恐」，見證人卻從未同意這項操作。官方備忘錄形容計畫「有影響力的」，秦墨不願把形容詞當作證據。

**EN 05**

An official version reached the room before the practical work was finished. Qin Mo read it once, then compared it with what they had actually measured. The difference was not always malicious, but it could still hurt somebody. Shen Lan asked that the correction be written in language the affected people could understand. The interview recorder captured words but not the years of rehearsal behind them. Shen Lan insisted on laboratory tests before a dramatic confession became a charge. A handwritten label read interaction, but no one could say who had written it there. The report claimed outcome, but Qin Mo wanted an example instead of a reassuring noun. One witness used the word primitive; Shen Lan noted that a description was not a measurement.

**繁中 05**

實際工作還沒完成，官方版本就傳到現場。秦墨讀了一次，再比對真正測得的結果。差異未必都是惡意，卻仍可能傷害人。沈嵐要求把更正寫成受影響者看得懂的語言。偵訊錄音留下話語，留不下背後多年的排練。沈嵐堅持在戲劇性認罪變成指控前先完成實驗室檢驗。手寫標籤寫著「相互作用」，卻沒有人知道是誰留下的。報告聲稱有「結果」，秦墨要求具體例子，而不是安撫人的名詞。一位證人用了「古早的」一詞，沈嵐指出形容並不等於測量。

**EN 06**

A witness asked whether the team was taking sides. Qin Mo answered that refusing to invent a fact was not the same as refusing to care. What mattered was whether the procedure protected someone whose story was less convenient to tell. Shen Lan listened without interrupting and wrote down the question exactly as it had been asked. Shen Lan noticed the word reception in a note that someone had added after the first inspection. A handwritten label read robber, but no one could say who had written it there. The term scenery appeared in the revised file; Qin Mo checked its meaning against the original page.

**繁中 06**

一名見證人問團隊是否偏袒。秦墨回答，不捏造事實不代表冷漠。真正重要的是程序能否保護那個故事不方便被說出口的人。沈嵐沒有打斷，逐字記下原問題。沈嵐注意到初次檢查後有人在筆記裡加上「接待會」一詞。手寫標籤寫著「強盜」，卻沒有人知道是誰留下的。修訂檔案出現「舞臺布景」一詞，秦墨拿原始頁面核對它的意思。

**EN 07**

Even after the group agreed on the immediate step, another question remained. The answer would not arrive through a dramatic announcement. Someone would have to return to the place, compare the earlier notes, and accept the possibility that the next piece of evidence might spoil an attractive theory. Qin Mo prepared to do exactly that. The station corridor sounded ordinary after midnight. Qin Mo found that the hardest part of investigating a powerful claim was giving an ordinary doubt enough time. The summary called the situation shameful; Qin Mo asked which observation justified that description. Shen Lan noticed the word terror in a note that someone had added after the first inspection. A handwritten label read witch, but no one could say who had written it there.

**繁中 07**

即使大家同意眼前步驟，另一個問題仍在。答案不會靠戲劇性公告降臨；有人得返回現場、比對舊筆記，並接受新證據可能推翻漂亮理論。秦墨準備做的正是這件事。午夜後警局走廊聲音平凡。秦墨發現調查強大宣稱時最難的是給平凡疑問足夠時間。摘要以「可恥的」形容情況，秦墨追問這種說法有何觀察根據。沈嵐注意到初次檢查後有人在筆記裡加上「恐懼」一詞。手寫標籤寫著「巫師」，卻沒有人知道是誰留下的。

An official memo described the plan as courageous, but Qin Mo refused to treat an adjective as evidence.

**繁中 03**

一場小爭執改變了時程。秦墨想趁證據消失前行動；沈嵐要求再核對一次，因為錯誤結論可能傷害難以自辯的人。他們沒有靠選領袖解決爭議，而是列出短暫、可逆測試真正能證明什麼。修訂檔案出現「列車長」一詞，秦墨拿原始頁面核對它的意思。沈嵐注意到初次檢查後有人在筆記裡加上「纜繩」一詞。官方備忘錄形容計畫「勇敢的」，秦墨不願把形容詞當作證據。

**EN 04**

One forged confession had put an innocent driver in prison, and Qin had endorsed it as a young detective. The plan had been amended three times, and each change left a trace. Qin Mo valued those corrections more than a perfect-looking first draft. If the outcome proved disappointing, the group would still be able to identify which assumption needed repair. Shen Lan watched for the quiet participant who had not yet been invited to speak. Shen Lan repeated the instruction to devise, then asked whether anyone had the right to refuse. The summary called the situation diligent; Qin Mo asked which observation justified that description. Shen Lan noticed the word dynasty in a note that someone had added after the first inspection.

**繁中 04**

一份偽造供詞曾使無辜司機入獄，年輕的秦墨當時背書支持。計畫改了三次，每次修改都留下痕跡。秦墨認為這些修正比看似完美的初稿更有價值。就算結果不理想，團隊仍能知道該修正哪一項假設。沈嵐也注意到那名一直沒有發言機會的人。沈嵐重述「想出」的指示，再問當事人是否有權拒絕。摘要以「勤勉的」形容情況，秦墨追問這種說法有何觀察根據。沈嵐注意到初次檢查後有人在筆記裡加上「世代；王朝」一詞。

**EN 05**

An official version reached the room before the practical work was finished. Qin Mo read it once, then compared it with what they had actually measured. The difference was not always malicious, but it could still hurt somebody. Shen Lan asked that the correction be written in language the affected people could understand. The station corridor sounded ordinary after midnight. Qin Mo found that the hardest part of investigating a powerful claim was giving an ordinary doubt enough time. Shen Lan noticed the word evaluation in a note that someone had added after the first inspection. A handwritten label read foam, but no one could say who had written it there. The term frequency appeared in the revised file; Qin Mo checked its meaning against the original page.

**繁中 05**

實際工作還沒完成，官方版本就傳到現場。秦墨讀了一次，再比對真正測得的結果。差異未必都是惡意，卻仍可能傷害人。沈嵐要求把更正寫成受影響者看得懂的語言。午夜後警局走廊聲音平凡。秦墨發現調查強大宣稱時最難的是給平凡疑問足夠時間。沈嵐注意到初次檢查後有人在筆記裡加上「評估」一詞。手寫標籤寫著「泡沫」，卻沒有人知道是誰留下的。修訂檔案出現「頻率」一詞，秦墨拿原始頁面核對它的意思。

**EN 06**

A witness asked whether the team was taking sides. Qin Mo answered that refusing to invent a fact was not the same as refusing to care. What mattered was whether the procedure protected someone whose story was less convenient to tell. Shen Lan listened without interrupting and wrote down the question exactly as it had been asked. The term imitation appeared in the revised file; Qin Mo checked its meaning against the original page. Shen Lan noticed the word labor in a note that someone had added after the first inspection. A handwritten label read mineral, but no one could say who had written it there.

**繁中 06**

一名見證人問團隊是否偏袒。秦墨回答，不捏造事實不代表冷漠。真正重要的是程序能否保護那個故事不方便被說出口的人。沈嵐沒有打斷，逐字記下原問題。修訂檔案出現「模仿」一詞，秦墨拿原始頁面核對它的意思。沈嵐注意到初次檢查後有人在筆記裡加上「體力勞動」一詞。手寫標籤寫著「礦物粉末中的礦物」，卻沒有人知道是誰留下的。

**EN 07**

Even after the group agreed on the immediate step, another question remained. The answer would not arrive through a dramatic announcement. Someone would have to return to the place, compare the earlier notes, and accept the possibility that the next piece of evidence might spoil an attractive theory. Qin Mo prepared to do exactly that. The interview recorder captured words but not the years of rehearsal behind them. Shen Lan insisted on laboratory tests before a dramatic confession became a charge. An official memo described the plan as needy, but Qin Mo refused to treat an adjective as evidence. The report claimed psychology, but Qin Mo wanted an example instead of a reassuring noun. On the next form, the action field read reflect, but the witness had never approved the operation.

**繁中 07**

即使大家同意眼前步驟，另一個問題仍在。答案不會靠戲劇性公告降臨；有人得返回現場、比對舊筆記，並接受新證據可能推翻漂亮理論。秦墨準備做的正是這件事。偵訊錄音留下話語，留不下背後多年的排練。沈嵐堅持在戲劇性認罪變成指控前先完成實驗室檢驗。官方備忘錄形容計畫「需要幫助的」，秦墨不願把形容詞當作證據。報告聲稱有「心理學；心理」，秦墨要求具體例子，而不是安撫人的名詞。下一張表單把動作寫成「反射；反思」，見證人卻從未同意這項操作。

**Q1. Which development is established in Chapter 6?**

**Q2. What new complication or decision changes the investigation in this chapter?**

A. All the witnesses agree to destroy their records.

B. The team abandons the people affected and declares a perfect victory.

**答案：C。** 一份偽造供詞曾使無辜司機入獄，年輕的秦墨當時背書支持。


## CHAPTER 07｜A Gift With a Blind Spot｜能力的盲點

### 正文與翻譯

**EN 01**

Shen Lan tested the vision on volunteers reading random phrases; Qin saw last planned lines, not necessarily the speakers' private intentions. Something had changed, but not everybody noticed it in the same order. Qin Mo listened to the account of the nearest worker, then to someone standing farther away. Their differences were not necessarily lies. Each person had been given a different view and a different reason to be afraid of admitting uncertainty. The interview recorder captured words but not the years of rehearsal behind them. Shen Lan insisted on laboratory tests before a dramatic confession became a charge. Shen Lan wrote accuracy in the margin and asked how the team would recognize it in practice. Shen Lan repeated the instruction to adapt, then asked whether anyone had the right to refuse.

**繁中 01**

沈嵐讓志工讀隨機片語測試能力，秦墨看見的是預定最後一句，而不一定是私下真心。某件事改變了，但人們發現它的順序不同。秦墨先聽近處工作人員的描述，再聽遠處旁觀者說法。差異不一定是謊言：每個人看到的角度不同，承認不確定時所要承擔的風險也不同。偵訊錄音留下話語，留不下背後多年的排練。沈嵐堅持在戲劇性認罪變成指控前先完成實驗室檢驗。沈嵐在頁邊寫下「準確度」，追問團隊實際上如何辨認。沈嵐重述「調整」的指示，再問當事人是否有權拒絕。

**EN 02**

One forged confession had put an innocent driver in prison, and Qin had endorsed it as a young detective. Qin Mo refused to pretend the previous decision had solved everything. The practical result was still there, useful but narrow. For each thing the team knew, there were two things it had not measured. Qin Mo drew a line through the claims that depended on speculation and asked Shen Lan to keep the original record intact. The instructions used the verb analyze without naming who would carry the risk; Qin Mo circled that gap. Shen Lan noticed the word competitor in a note that someone had added after the first inspection. The earlier photograph clearly showed a diploma; the newer version did not, so Qin Mo kept both.

**繁中 02**

一份偽造供詞曾使無辜司機入獄，年輕的秦墨當時背書支持。秦墨不願假裝前一次決定已解決所有問題。成果確實存在，卻有明確界限。每個已知事項旁都還有未測量的部分。秦墨劃掉靠猜測成立的說法，請沈嵐保留原始紀錄。指示裡用了「分析」這個動詞，卻沒說誰承擔風險；秦墨圈出這項缺漏。沈嵐注意到初次檢查後有人在筆記裡加上「競爭者」一詞。較早的照片清楚顯示一項「文憑」，新版卻沒有，秦墨因此保留兩張。

**EN 03**

A small disagreement changed the schedule. Qin Mo wanted to proceed before evidence disappeared; Shen Lan wanted another check because a wrong conclusion might hurt people who could not easily defend themselves. They did not settle the matter by choosing a leader. They wrote down what a short, reversible test could actually establish. The earlier photograph clearly showed a frost; the newer version did not, so Qin Mo kept both. A note about the helicopter lay beside the old register, with a date that Qin Mo could not verify. Shen Lan pointed to the herd in the inventory and asked why its location had changed.

**繁中 03**

一場小爭執改變了時程。秦墨想趁證據消失前行動；沈嵐要求再核對一次，因為錯誤結論可能傷害難以自辯的人。他們沒有靠選領袖解決爭議，而是列出短暫、可逆測試真正能證明什麼。較早的照片清楚顯示一項「霜凍」，新版卻沒有，秦墨因此保留兩張。一張談到「直升機」的便條放在舊登記簿旁，日期讓秦墨無法核實。沈嵐指向清單上的「羊群」，追問其位置為何改變。

**EN 04**

A suspect could deliberately feed a dramatic sentence into Qin's sight, making the gift a channel for deception. The plan had been amended three times, and each change left a trace. Qin Mo valued those corrections more than a perfect-looking first draft. If the outcome proved disappointing, the group would still be able to identify which assumption needed repair. Shen Lan watched for the quiet participant who had not yet been invited to speak. On the next form, the action field read injure, but the witness had never approved the operation. An official memo described the plan as intense, but Qin Mo refused to treat an adjective as evidence. A note about the mule lay beside the old register, with a date that Qin Mo could not verify.

**繁中 04**

嫌疑人能故意把戲劇性的句子送入秦墨視野，使能力成為欺騙管道。計畫改了三次，每次修改都留下痕跡。秦墨認為這些修正比看似完美的初稿更有價值。就算結果不理想，團隊仍能知道該修正哪一項假設。沈嵐也注意到那名一直沒有發言機會的人。下一張表單把動作寫成「使受傷」，見證人卻從未同意這項操作。官方備忘錄形容計畫「激烈的」，秦墨不願把形容詞當作證據。一張談到「騾子」的便條放在舊登記簿旁，日期讓秦墨無法核實。

The difference was not always malicious, but it could still hurt somebody. Shen Lan asked that the correction be written in language the affected people could understand. The summary called the situation mutual; Qin Mo asked which observation justified that description. On the next form, the action field read oppose, but the witness had never approved the operation. **繁中 05**

**EN 06**

A witness asked whether the team was taking sides. Qin Mo answered that refusing to invent a fact was not the same as refusing to care. 一名見證人問團隊是否偏袒。秦墨回答，不捏造事實不代表冷漠。真正重要的是程序能否保護那個故事不方便被說出口的人。沈嵐沒有打斷，逐字記下原問題。手寫標籤寫著「水管工」，卻沒有人知道是誰留下的。修訂檔案出現「減少」一詞，秦墨拿原始頁面核對它的意思。一位證人用了「不尋常的；顯著的」一詞，沈嵐指出形容並不等於測量。

**EN 07**

Even after the group agreed on the immediate step, another question remained. The answer would not arrive through a dramatic announcement. Someone would have to return to the place, compare the earlier notes, and accept the possibility that the next piece of evidence might spoil an attractive theory. Qin Mo prepared to do exactly that. The station corridor sounded ordinary after midnight. Qin Mo found that the hardest part of investigating a powerful claim was giving an ordinary doubt enough time. Shen Lan noticed the word slight in a note that someone had added after the first inspection. A handwritten label read surroundings, but no one could say who had written it there. ## CHAPTER 08｜The Second Death｜第二起死亡

### 正文與翻譯

**EN 01**

A former assistant of the speech coach died after a rehearsal. Her notebook contained corrections to the confession in the driver's old case. Something had changed, but not everybody noticed it in the same order. Qin Mo listened to the account of the nearest worker, then to someone standing farther away. Their differences were not necessarily lies. Each person had been given a different view and a different reason to be afraid of admitting uncertainty. The station corridor sounded ordinary after midnight. Qin Mo found that the hardest part of investigating a powerful claim was giving an ordinary doubt enough time. The term ancestor appeared in the revised file; Qin Mo checked its meaning against the original page. Shen Lan noticed the word contrary in a note that someone had added after the first inspection.

**繁中 01**

語言教練的前助理在排練後身亡，她的筆記本保留了司機舊案供詞的修改痕跡。某件事改變了，但人們發現它的順序不同。秦墨先聽近處工作人員的描述，再聽遠處旁觀者說法。差異不一定是謊言：每個人看到的角度不同，承認不確定時所要承擔的風險也不同。午夜後警局走廊聲音平凡。秦墨發現調查強大宣稱時最難的是給平凡疑問足夠時間。修訂檔案出現「祖先」一詞，秦墨拿原始頁面核對它的意思。沈嵐注意到初次檢查後有人在筆記裡加上「正好相反」一詞。

**EN 02**

A suspect could deliberately feed a dramatic sentence into Qin's sight, making the gift a channel for deception. Qin Mo refused to pretend the previous decision had solved everything. The practical result was still there, useful but narrow. For each thing the team knew, there were two things it had not measured. Qin Mo drew a line through the claims that depended on speculation and asked Shen Lan to keep the original record intact. A handwritten label read craft, but no one could say who had written it there. The term crush appeared in the revised file; Qin Mo checked its meaning against the original page. One witness used the word defensible; Shen Lan noted that a description was not a measurement.

**繁中 02**

嫌疑人能故意把戲劇性的句子送入秦墨視野，使能力成為欺騙管道。秦墨不願假裝前一次決定已解決所有問題。成果確實存在，卻有明確界限。每個已知事項旁都還有未測量的部分。秦墨劃掉靠猜測成立的說法，請沈嵐保留原始紀錄。手寫標籤寫著「手工技藝」，卻沒有人知道是誰留下的。修訂檔案出現「擠壓；造成壓傷」一詞，秦墨拿原始頁面核對它的意思。一位證人用了「有充分理由的」一詞，沈嵐指出形容並不等於測量。

**EN 03**

A small disagreement changed the schedule. Qin Mo wanted to proceed before evidence disappeared; Shen Lan wanted another check because a wrong conclusion might hurt people who could not easily defend themselves. They did not settle the matter by choosing a leader. They wrote down what a short, reversible test could actually establish. One witness used the word distinguished; Shen Lan noted that a description was not a measurement. A handwritten label read equip, but no one could say who had written it there. The term guilt appeared in the revised file; Qin Mo checked its meaning against the original page.

**繁中 03**

一場小爭執改變了時程。秦墨想趁證據消失前行動；沈嵐要求再核對一次，因為錯誤結論可能傷害難以自辯的人。他們沒有靠選領袖解決爭議，而是列出短暫、可逆測試真正能證明什麼。一位證人用了「傑出的；精湛的」一詞，沈嵐指出形容並不等於測量。手寫標籤寫著「器材」，卻沒有人知道是誰留下的。修訂檔案出現「愧疚」一詞，秦墨拿原始頁面核對它的意思。

**EN 04**

The new death shared a chemical trace with the first, but the delivery mechanisms were different. The plan had been amended three times, and each change left a trace. Qin Mo valued those corrections more than a perfect-looking first draft. If the outcome proved disappointing, the group would still be able to identify which assumption needed repair. Shen Lan watched for the quiet participant who had not yet been invited to speak. A note about the handwriting lay beside the old register, with a date that Qin Mo could not verify. Shen Lan pointed to the hose in the inventory and asked why its location had changed. An official memo described the plan as identical, but Qin Mo refused to treat an adjective as evidence.

**繁中 04**

新案件與首案有相同化學痕跡，施用方式卻不同。計畫改了三次，每次修改都留下痕跡。秦墨認為這些修正比看似完美的初稿更有價值。就算結果不理想，團隊仍能知道該修正哪一項假設。沈嵐也注意到那名一直沒有發言機會的人。一張談到「字跡」的便條放在舊登記簿旁，日期讓秦墨無法核實。沈嵐指向清單上的「水管」，追問其位置為何改變。官方備忘錄形容計畫「完全相同的」，秦墨不願把形容詞當作證據。

**EN 05**

An official version reached the room before the practical work was finished. Qin Mo read it once, then compared it with what they had actually measured. The difference was not always malicious, but it could still hurt somebody. Shen Lan asked that the correction be written in language the affected people could understand. The station corridor sounded ordinary after midnight. Qin Mo found that the hardest part of investigating a powerful claim was giving an ordinary doubt enough time. A handwritten label read involve, but no one could say who had written it there. The term launch appeared in the revised file; Qin Mo checked its meaning against the original page. One witness used the word lousy; Shen Lan noted that a description was not a measurement.

**繁中 05**

實際工作還沒完成，官方版本就傳到現場。秦墨讀了一次，再比對真正測得的結果。差異未必都是惡意，卻仍可能傷害人。沈嵐要求把更正寫成受影響者看得懂的語言。午夜後警局走廊聲音平凡。秦墨發現調查強大宣稱時最難的是給平凡疑問足夠時間。手寫標籤寫著「涉及；參與」，卻沒有人知道是誰留下的。修訂檔案出現「推出；啟動」一詞，秦墨拿原始頁面核對它的意思。一位證人用了「品質差的」一詞，沈嵐指出形容並不等於測量。

**EN 06**

A witness asked whether the team was taking sides. Qin Mo answered that refusing to invent a fact was not the same as refusing to care. What mattered was whether the procedure protected someone whose story was less convenient to tell. Shen Lan listened without interrupting and wrote down the question exactly as it had been asked. On the next form, the action field read misunderstand, but the witness had never approved the operation. The earlier photograph clearly showed a pregnancy; the newer version did not, so Qin Mo kept both. The term riddle appeared in the revised file; Qin Mo checked its meaning against the original page.

**繁中 06**

一名見證人問團隊是否偏袒。秦墨回答，不捏造事實不代表冷漠。真正重要的是程序能否保護那個故事不方便被說出口的人。沈嵐沒有打斷，逐字記下原問題。下一張表單把動作寫成「誤會」，見證人卻從未同意這項操作。較早的照片清楚顯示一項「懷孕」，新版卻沒有，秦墨因此保留兩張。修訂檔案出現「謎題」一詞，秦墨拿原始頁面核對它的意思。

**EN 07**

Even after the group agreed on the immediate step, another question remained. The answer would not arrive through a dramatic announcement. Someone would have to return to the place, compare the earlier notes, and accept the possibility that the next piece of evidence might spoil an attractive theory. Qin Mo prepared to do exactly that. The interview recorder captured words but not the years of rehearsal behind them. Shen Lan insisted on laboratory tests before a dramatic confession became a charge. The term stroke appeared in the revised file; Qin Mo checked its meaning against the original page. Shen Lan noticed the word suggestion in a note that someone had added after the first inspection. A handwritten label read technician, but no one could say who had written it there.

**繁中 07**

即使大家同意眼前步驟，另一個問題仍在。答案不會靠戲劇性公告降臨；有人得返回現場、比對舊筆記，並接受新證據可能推翻漂亮理論。秦墨準備做的正是這件事。偵訊錄音留下話語，留不下背後多年的排練。沈嵐堅持在戲劇性認罪變成指控前先完成實驗室檢驗。修訂檔案出現「一筆」一詞，秦墨拿原始頁面核對它的意思。沈嵐注意到初次檢查後有人在筆記裡加上「建議」一詞。手寫標籤寫著「技術員」，卻沒有人知道是誰留下的。

### 理解測驗

**Q1. Which development is established in Chapter 8?**

A. The investigators decide that every earlier warning was fictional.

B. A former assistant of the speech coach died after a rehearsal. Her notebook contained corrections to the confession in the driver's old case.

C. The central problem disappears without further action.

**答案：B。** 語言教練的前助理在排練後身亡，她的筆記本保留了司機舊案供詞的修改痕跡。

**Q2. What new complication or decision changes the investigation in this chapter?**

A. All the witnesses agree to destroy their records.

B. The team abandons the people affected and declares a perfect victory.

C. The new death shared a chemical trace with the first, but the delivery mechanisms were different.

**答案：C。** 新案件與首案有相同化學痕跡，施用方式卻不同。


## CHAPTER 09｜The Words She Never Spoke｜她沒說出口的話

### 正文與翻譯

**EN 01**

An actress testified that the coach had forced her to memorize accusations against a stranger, then promised to delete the recording. Something had changed, but not everybody noticed it in the same order. Qin Mo listened to the account of the nearest worker, then to someone standing farther away. Their differences were not necessarily lies. Each person had been given a different view and a different reason to be afraid of admitting uncertainty. The interview recorder captured words but not the years of rehearsal behind them. Shen Lan insisted on laboratory tests before a dramatic confession became a charge. Shen Lan repeated the instruction to arise, then asked whether anyone had the right to refuse. The term association appeared in the revised file; Qin Mo checked its meaning against the original page.

**繁中 01**

一名演員表示教練迫使她背誦對陌生人的指控，之後答應刪除錄音。某件事改變了，但人們發現它的順序不同。秦墨先聽近處工作人員的描述，再聽遠處旁觀者說法。差異不一定是謊言：每個人看到的角度不同，承認不確定時所要承擔的風險也不同。偵訊錄音留下話語，留不下背後多年的排練。沈嵐堅持在戲劇性認罪變成指控前先完成實驗室檢驗。沈嵐重述「產生」的指示，再問當事人是否有權拒絕。修訂檔案出現「協會」一詞，秦墨拿原始頁面核對它的意思。

**EN 02**

The new death shared a chemical trace with the first, but the delivery mechanisms were different. Qin Mo refused to pretend the previous decision had solved everything. The practical result was still there, useful but narrow. For each thing the team knew, there were two things it had not measured. Qin Mo drew a line through the claims that depended on speculation and asked Shen Lan to keep the original record intact. Shen Lan noticed the word autobiography in a note that someone had added after the first inspection. An official memo described the plan as bald, but Qin Mo refused to treat an adjective as evidence. The summary called the situation consequent; Qin Mo asked which observation justified that description.

**繁中 02**

新案件與首案有相同化學痕跡，施用方式卻不同。秦墨不願假裝前一次決定已解決所有問題。成果確實存在，卻有明確界限。每個已知事項旁都還有未測量的部分。秦墨劃掉靠猜測成立的說法，請沈嵐保留原始紀錄。沈嵐注意到初次檢查後有人在筆記裡加上「自傳」一詞。官方備忘錄形容計畫「光頭的」，秦墨不願把形容詞當作證據。摘要以「隨之而來的」形容情況，秦墨追問這種說法有何觀察根據。

A small disagreement changed the schedule. | `L4-0609` | `machinery` | n. | `machinery` | 機械設備 | The earlier photograph clearly showed a machinery; the newer version did not, so Qin Mo kept both. |

### 理解測驗

**Q1. Which development is established in Chapter 9?**

A. The investigators decide that every earlier warning was fictional.

B. An actress testified that the coach had forced her to memorize accusations against a stranger, then promised to delete the recording.

C. The central problem disappears without further action.

**答案：B。** 一名演員表示教練迫使她背誦對陌生人的指控，之後答應刪除錄音。

**Q2. What new complication or decision changes the investigation in this chapter?**

A. All the witnesses agree to destroy their records.

B. The team abandons the people affected and declares a perfect victory.

C. She recognized the phrase THE KILLER IS YOU as a line from an unfinished rehearsal script.

**答案：C。** 她認出「兇手是你」出自一份尚未完成的排練劇本。


## CHAPTER 10｜The Script Beneath the Floor｜地板下的劇本

### 正文與翻譯

**EN 01**

Investigators recovered a carbon-copy script beneath a studio floor. Its page numbers showed that several people had rehearsed the same accusation. Something had changed, but not everybody noticed it in the same order. Qin Mo listened to the account of the nearest worker, then to someone standing farther away. Their differences were not necessarily lies. Each person had been given a different view and a different reason to be afraid of admitting uncertainty. The station corridor sounded ordinary after midnight. Qin Mo found that the hardest part of investigating a powerful claim was giving an ordinary doubt enough time. Shen Lan wrote acceptance in the margin and asked how the team would recognize it in practice. An official memo described the plan as ambitious, but Qin Mo refused to treat an adjective as evidence.

**繁中 01**

調查人員在攝影棚地板下找到複寫劇本，頁碼顯示多人練過同一句指控。某件事改變了，但人們發現它的順序不同。秦墨先聽近處工作人員的描述，再聽遠處旁觀者說法。差異不一定是謊言：每個人看到的角度不同，承認不確定時所要承擔的風險也不同。午夜後警局走廊聲音平凡。秦墨發現調查強大宣稱時最難的是給平凡疑問足夠時間。沈嵐在頁邊寫下「接受；同意」，追問團隊實際上如何辨認。官方備忘錄形容計畫「有企圖心的」，秦墨不願把形容詞當作證據。

**EN 02**

She recognized the phrase THE KILLER IS YOU as a line from an unfinished rehearsal script. Qin Mo refused to pretend the previous decision had solved everything. The practical result was still there, useful but narrow. For each thing the team knew, there were two things it had not measured. Qin Mo drew a line through the claims that depended on speculation and asked Shen Lan to keep the original record intact. The term atmosphere appeared in the revised file; Qin Mo checked its meaning against the original page. One witness used the word authentic; Shen Lan noted that a description was not a measurement. Shen Lan repeated the instruction to classify, then asked whether anyone had the right to refuse.

**繁中 02**

她認出「兇手是你」出自一份尚未完成的排練劇本。秦墨不願假裝前一次決定已解決所有問題。成果確實存在，卻有明確界限。每個已知事項旁都還有未測量的部分。秦墨劃掉靠猜測成立的說法，請沈嵐保留原始紀錄。修訂檔案出現「氣氛」一詞，秦墨拿原始頁面核對它的意思。一位證人用了「真實可靠的」一詞，沈嵐指出形容並不等於測量。沈嵐重述「分類」的指示，再問當事人是否有權拒絕。

**EN 03**

A small disagreement changed the schedule. Qin Mo wanted to proceed before evidence disappeared; Shen Lan wanted another check because a wrong conclusion might hurt people who could not easily defend themselves. They did not settle the matter by choosing a leader. They wrote down what a short, reversible test could actually establish. Shen Lan repeated the instruction to differ, then asked whether anyone had the right to refuse. The report claimed dignity, but Qin Mo wanted an example instead of a reassuring noun. Shen Lan noticed the word embarrass in a note that someone had added after the first inspection.

**繁中 03**

一場小爭執改變了時程。秦墨想趁證據消失前行動；沈嵐要求再核對一次，因為錯誤結論可能傷害難以自辯的人。他們沒有靠選領袖解決爭議，而是列出短暫、可逆測試真正能證明什麼。沈嵐重述「有所不同」的指示，再問當事人是否有權拒絕。報告聲稱有「尊嚴」，秦墨要求具體例子，而不是安撫人的名詞。沈嵐注意到初次檢查後有人在筆記裡加上「使人難堪」一詞。

**EN 04**

The final page addressed Qin by name and asked him to investigate the innocent driver he had helped convict. The plan had been amended three times, and each change left a trace. Qin Mo valued those corrections more than a perfect-looking first draft. If the outcome proved disappointing, the group would still be able to identify which assumption needed repair. Shen Lan watched for the quiet participant who had not yet been invited to speak. Shen Lan noticed the word facility in a note that someone had added after the first inspection. An official memo described the plan as genuine, but Qin Mo refused to treat an adjective as evidence. The summary called the situation offensive; Qin Mo asked which observation justified that description.

**繁中 04**

最後一頁直接點名秦墨，要他重新調查自己曾協助定罪的無辜司機。計畫改了三次，每次修改都留下痕跡。秦墨認為這些修正比看似完美的初稿更有價值。就算結果不理想，團隊仍能知道該修正哪一項假設。沈嵐也注意到那名一直沒有發言機會的人。沈嵐注意到初次檢查後有人在筆記裡加上「設施」一詞。官方備忘錄形容計畫「真實的」，秦墨不願把形容詞當作證據。摘要以「惡意的」形容情況，秦墨追問這種說法有何觀察根據。

**EN 05**

An official version reached the room before the practical work was finished. Qin Mo read it once, then compared it with what they had actually measured. The difference was not always malicious, but it could still hurt somebody. Shen Lan asked that the correction be written in language the affected people could understand. The station corridor sounded ordinary after midnight. Qin Mo found that the hardest part of investigating a powerful claim was giving an ordinary doubt enough time. The term option appeared in the revised file; Qin Mo checked its meaning against the original page. Shen Lan noticed the word perfume in a note that someone had added after the first inspection. A handwritten label read promotion, but no one could say who had written it there.

**繁中 05**

實際工作還沒完成，官方版本就傳到現場。秦墨讀了一次，再比對真正測得的結果。差異未必都是惡意，卻仍可能傷害人。沈嵐要求把更正寫成受影響者看得懂的語言。午夜後警局走廊聲音平凡。秦墨發現調查強大宣稱時最難的是給平凡疑問足夠時間。修訂檔案出現「選項」一詞，秦墨拿原始頁面核對它的意思。沈嵐注意到初次檢查後有人在筆記裡加上「香水」一詞。手寫標籤寫著「行銷」，卻沒有人知道是誰留下的。

**EN 06**

A witness asked whether the team was taking sides. Qin Mo answered that refusing to invent a fact was not the same as refusing to care. What mattered was whether the procedure protected someone whose story was less convenient to tell. Shen Lan listened without interrupting and wrote down the question exactly as it had been asked. A handwritten label read publisher, but no one could say who had written it there. The term refugee appeared in the revised file; Qin Mo checked its meaning against the original page. Shen Lan noticed the word rescue in a note that someone had added after the first inspection.

**繁中 06**

一名見證人問團隊是否偏袒。秦墨回答，不捏造事實不代表冷漠。真正重要的是程序能否保護那個故事不方便被說出口的人。沈嵐沒有打斷，逐字記下原問題。手寫標籤寫著「出版社」，卻沒有人知道是誰留下的。修訂檔案出現「難民」一詞，秦墨拿原始頁面核對它的意思。沈嵐注意到初次檢查後有人在筆記裡加上「救援」一詞。

**EN 07**

The last page of the script carried a resignation note from a studio assistant and a drawing of a Christmas stocking hung upside down over an interrogation chair. Qin Mo turned it slowly beneath the lamp. The page did not identify a killer. It did something worse: it reproduced a line he himself had written years earlier when a coerced confession helped convict an innocent driver. Whoever had assembled this file wanted to transform his old guilt into the next headline. Shen Lan touched the paper's lower corner. There, in a different hand, was a date one day before the speech coach's death. 'Someone prepared this long before the murder,' she said. Qin looked toward the dark recording booth. A red light blinked on, although the power had been cut.

**繁中 07**

劇本最後一頁附著攝影棚助理的辭職便條，還畫了倒掛在偵訊椅上的聖誕長襪。秦墨在燈下慢慢轉動紙張。它沒有指認兇手，反而做了更可怕的事：重現他多年前在一樁逼供案件中寫下的句子，當時無辜司機因此入獄。編造這份文件的人，想把他的舊罪咎轉化成下一則新聞標題。沈嵐摸了摸紙張下角，那裡用另一種筆跡寫著日期：語言教練死亡前一天。「有人在命案之前就準備好了。」她說。秦墨看向黑暗的錄音間。明明電源已切斷，紅燈卻亮了。

**答案：B。** 調查人員在攝影棚地板下找到複寫劇本，頁碼顯示多人練過同一句指控。

Shen Lan noticed the word assurance in a note that someone had added after the first inspection.

**Q1. Which development is established in Chapter 11?**

A. The investigators decide that every earlier warning was fictional.

B. One suspect confessed to the first killing on camera, but the timing of his movements excluded him from the actual crime scene.

C. The central problem disappears without further action.

**答案：B。** 一名嫌疑人對鏡頭認罪，行動時間卻排除他親自在場行兇的可能。

**Q2. What new complication or decision changes the investigation in this chapter?**

A. All the witnesses agree to destroy their records.

B. The team abandons the people affected and declares a perfect victory.

C. Qin rejected the convenient confession and asked who benefited from a false arrest at this moment.

**答案：C。** 秦墨拒絕方便的認罪，追問現在誰會從錯捕中獲利。


## CHAPTER 12｜A Clock Out of Rhythm｜不合節拍的鐘

### 正文與翻譯

**EN 01**

Shen Lan compared studio recordings against a mechanical clock that lost seconds during each rehearsal of the final line. Something had changed, but not everybody noticed it in the same order. Qin Mo listened to the account of the nearest worker, then to someone standing farther away. Their differences were not necessarily lies. Each person had been given a different view and a different reason to be afraid of admitting uncertainty. The station corridor sounded ordinary after midnight. Qin Mo found that the hardest part of investigating a powerful claim was giving an ordinary doubt enough time. An official memo described the plan as absolute, but Qin Mo refused to treat an adjective as evidence. The report claimed discipline, but Qin Mo wanted an example instead of a reassuring noun.

**繁中 01**

沈嵐比對攝影棚錄音與每次排練最後一句都會慢幾秒的機械鐘。某件事改變了，但人們發現它的順序不同。秦墨先聽近處工作人員的描述，再聽遠處旁觀者說法。差異不一定是謊言：每個人看到的角度不同，承認不確定時所要承擔的風險也不同。午夜後警局走廊聲音平凡。秦墨發現調查強大宣稱時最難的是給平凡疑問足夠時間。官方備忘錄形容計畫「絕對的」，秦墨不願把形容詞當作證據。報告聲稱有「紀律」，秦墨要求具體例子，而不是安撫人的名詞。

**EN 02**

Qin rejected the convenient confession and asked who benefited from a false arrest at this moment. Qin Mo refused to pretend the previous decision had solved everything. The practical result was still there, useful but narrow. For each thing the team knew, there were two things it had not measured. Qin Mo drew a line through the claims that depended on speculation and asked Shen Lan to keep the original record intact. Shen Lan noticed the word distribution in a note that someone had added after the first inspection. A handwritten label read dread, but no one could say who had written it there. The report claimed efficiency, but Qin Mo wanted an example instead of a reassuring noun.

**繁中 02**

秦墨拒絕方便的認罪，追問現在誰會從錯捕中獲利。秦墨不願假裝前一次決定已解決所有問題。成果確實存在，卻有明確界限。每個已知事項旁都還有未測量的部分。秦墨劃掉靠猜測成立的說法，請沈嵐保留原始紀錄。沈嵐注意到初次檢查後有人在筆記裡加上「分布」一詞。手寫標籤寫著「恐懼」，卻沒有人知道是誰留下的。報告聲稱有「效率」，秦墨要求具體例子，而不是安撫人的名詞。

**EN 03**

A small disagreement changed the schedule. Qin Mo wanted to proceed before evidence disappeared; Shen Lan wanted another check because a wrong conclusion might hurt people who could not easily defend themselves. They did not settle the matter by choosing a leader. They wrote down what a short, reversible test could actually establish. The instructions used the verb fasten without naming who would carry the risk; Qin Mo circled that gap. One witness used the word gracious; Shen Lan noted that a description was not a measurement. A handwritten label read grief, but no one could say who had written it there.

**繁中 03**

一場小爭執改變了時程。秦墨想趁證據消失前行動；沈嵐要求再核對一次，因為錯誤結論可能傷害難以自辯的人。他們沒有靠選領袖解決爭議，而是列出短暫、可逆測試真正能證明什麼。指示裡用了「固定」這個動詞，卻沒說誰承擔風險；秦墨圈出這項缺漏。一位證人用了「親切的」一詞，沈嵐指出形容並不等於測量。手寫標籤寫著「悲傷」，卻沒有人知道是誰留下的。

**EN 04**

The apparent murder time had been moved by edited sound, not by a real shift in the victim's death. The plan had been amended three times, and each change left a trace. Qin Mo valued those corrections more than a perfect-looking first draft. If the outcome proved disappointing, the group would still be able to identify which assumption needed repair. Shen Lan watched for the quiet participant who had not yet been invited to speak. A handwritten label read hardware, but no one could say who had written it there. The term hawk appeared in the revised file; Qin Mo checked its meaning against the original page. Shen Lan noticed the word illustration in a note that someone had added after the first inspection.

**繁中 04**

表面死亡時間是由剪輯音效移動，而非死者真正的死亡時間。計畫改了三次，每次修改都留下痕跡。秦墨認為這些修正比看似完美的初稿更有價值。就算結果不理想，團隊仍能知道該修正哪一項假設。沈嵐也注意到那名一直沒有發言機會的人。手寫標籤寫著「電子硬體」，卻沒有人知道是誰留下的。修訂檔案出現「老鷹」一詞，秦墨拿原始頁面核對它的意思。沈嵐注意到初次檢查後有人在筆記裡加上「插圖」一詞。

**EN 05**

An official version reached the room before the practical work was finished. Qin Mo read it once, then compared it with what they had actually measured. The difference was not always malicious, but it could still hurt somebody. Shen Lan asked that the correction be written in language the affected people could understand. The station corridor sounded ordinary after midnight. Qin Mo found that the hardest part of investigating a powerful claim was giving an ordinary doubt enough time. Shen Lan noticed the word insert in a note that someone had added after the first inspection. A handwritten label read miner, but no one could say who had written it there. The term oval appeared in the revised file; Qin Mo checked its meaning against the original page.

**繁中 05**

實際工作還沒完成，官方版本就傳到現場。秦墨讀了一次，再比對真正測得的結果。差異未必都是惡意，卻仍可能傷害人。沈嵐要求把更正寫成受影響者看得懂的語言。午夜後警局走廊聲音平凡。秦墨發現調查強大宣稱時最難的是給平凡疑問足夠時間。沈嵐注意到初次檢查後有人在筆記裡加上「插入」一詞。手寫標籤寫著「礦工」，卻沒有人知道是誰留下的。修訂檔案出現「橢圓形的」一詞，秦墨拿原始頁面核對它的意思。

**EN 06**

A witness asked whether the team was taking sides. Qin Mo answered that refusing to invent a fact was not the same as refusing to care. What mattered was whether the procedure protected someone whose story was less convenient to tell. Shen Lan listened without interrupting and wrote down the question exactly as it had been asked. The term perfection appeared in the revised file; Qin Mo checked its meaning against the original page. One witness used the word realistic; Shen Lan noted that a description was not a measurement. People disagreed about recognition; Qin Mo separated the feeling from the facts they could test.

**繁中 06**

一名見證人問團隊是否偏袒。秦墨回答，不捏造事實不代表冷漠。真正重要的是程序能否保護那個故事不方便被說出口的人。沈嵐沒有打斷，逐字記下原問題。修訂檔案出現「完美」一詞，秦墨拿原始頁面核對它的意思。一位證人用了「實際可行的」一詞，沈嵐指出形容並不等於測量。大家對「辨識」意見不同，秦墨把感受與可檢驗的事實分開。

**EN 07**

Even after the group agreed on the immediate step, another question remained. The answer would not arrive through a dramatic announcement. Someone would have to return to the place, compare the earlier notes, and accept the possibility that the next piece of evidence might spoil an attractive theory. Qin Mo prepared to do exactly that. The interview recorder captured words but not the years of rehearsal behind them. Shen Lan insisted on laboratory tests before a dramatic confession became a charge. Shen Lan repeated the instruction to renew, then asked whether anyone had the right to refuse. The term resolve appeared in the revised file; Qin Mo checked its meaning against the original page. Shen Lan noticed the word vessel in a note that someone had added after the first inspection.

**繁中 07**

即使大家同意眼前步驟，另一個問題仍在。答案不會靠戲劇性公告降臨；有人得返回現場、比對舊筆記，並接受新證據可能推翻漂亮理論。秦墨準備做的正是這件事。偵訊錄音留下話語，留不下背後多年的排練。沈嵐堅持在戲劇性認罪變成指控前先完成實驗室檢驗。沈嵐重述「更新；恢復」的指示，再問當事人是否有權拒絕。修訂檔案出現「決定」一詞，秦墨拿原始頁面核對它的意思。沈嵐注意到初次檢查後有人在筆記裡加上「容器」一詞。

實際工作還沒完成，官方版本就傳到現場。秦墨讀了一次，再比對真正測得的結果。差異未必都是惡意，卻仍可能傷害人。沈嵐要求把更正寫成受影響者看得懂的語言。偵訊錄音留下話語，留不下背後多年的排練。沈嵐堅持在戲劇性認罪變成指控前先完成實驗室檢驗。摘要以「令人難忘的」形容情況，秦墨追問這種說法有何觀察根據。一位證人用了「悲慘的」一詞，沈嵐指出形容並不等於測量。手寫標籤寫著「氧氣」，卻沒有人知道是誰留下的。

**EN 06**

A witness asked whether the team was taking sides. Qin Mo answered that refusing to invent a fact was not the same as refusing to care. What mattered was whether the procedure protected someone whose story was less convenient to tell. Shen Lan listened without interrupting and wrote down the question exactly as it had been asked. A handwritten label read proposal, but no one could say who had written it there. The instructions used the verb scarcely without naming who would carry the risk; Qin Mo circled that gap. Shen Lan noticed the word sketch in a note that someone had added after the first inspection.

**繁中 06**

一名見證人問團隊是否偏袒。秦墨回答，不捏造事實不代表冷漠。真正重要的是程序能否保護那個故事不方便被說出口的人。沈嵐沒有打斷，逐字記下原問題。手寫標籤寫著「提案」，卻沒有人知道是誰留下的。指示裡用了「幾乎不」這個動詞，卻沒說誰承擔風險；秦墨圈出這項缺漏。沈嵐注意到初次檢查後有人在筆記裡加上「草圖」一詞。

**EN 07**

Even after the group agreed on the immediate step, another question remained. The answer would not arrive through a dramatic announcement. Someone would have to return to the place, compare the earlier notes, and accept the possibility that the next piece of evidence might spoil an attractive theory. Qin Mo prepared to do exactly that. The station corridor sounded ordinary after midnight. Qin Mo found that the hardest part of investigating a powerful claim was giving an ordinary doubt enough time. Shen Lan noticed the word stripe in a note that someone had added after the first inspection. Shen Lan repeated the instruction to summarize, then asked whether anyone had the right to refuse. The term universal appeared in the revised file; Qin Mo checked its meaning against the original page.

**繁中 07**

即使大家同意眼前步驟，另一個問題仍在。答案不會靠戲劇性公告降臨；有人得返回現場、比對舊筆記，並接受新證據可能推翻漂亮理論。秦墨準備做的正是這件事。午夜後警局走廊聲音平凡。秦墨發現調查強大宣稱時最難的是給平凡疑問足夠時間。沈嵐注意到初次檢查後有人在筆記裡加上「條紋」一詞。沈嵐重述「整理」的指示，再問當事人是否有權拒絕。修訂檔案出現「通用的」一詞，秦墨拿原始頁面核對它的意思。

**Q1. Which development is established in Chapter 13?**

A. The investigators decide that every earlier warning was fictional.

B. The wrongly convicted driver was released under review. He refused to become an easy symbol for the police department's apology campaign.

C. The central problem disappears without further action.

**答案：B。** 冤獄司機獲准在覆審期間離開監所，拒絕成為警局道歉宣傳的象徵。

**Q2. What new complication or decision changes the investigation in this chapter?**

A. All the witnesses agree to destroy their records.

B. The team abandons the people affected and declares a perfect victory.

C. He handed Qin a letter naming the person who had paid for the original forged confession.

**答案：C。** 他交給秦墨一封信，寫明當年支付偽造供詞費用的人。


## CHAPTER 14｜The Producer's Insurance｜製作人的保險

### 正文與翻譯

**EN 01**

A producer had insured the speech coach's archive and stood to profit if scandal destroyed its remaining copies. Something had changed, but not everybody noticed it in the same order. Qin Mo listened to the account of the nearest worker, then to someone standing farther away. Their differences were not necessarily lies. Each person had been given a different view and a different reason to be afraid of admitting uncertainty. The station corridor sounded ordinary after midnight. Qin Mo found that the hardest part of investigating a powerful claim was giving an ordinary doubt enough time. The term access appeared in the revised file; Qin Mo checked its meaning against the original page. Shen Lan noticed the word acquaintance in a note that someone had added after the first inspection.

**繁中 01**

某製作人替語言教練的檔案投保，醜聞毀掉最後副本時，他反而可能獲利。某件事改變了，但人們發現它的順序不同。秦墨先聽近處工作人員的描述，再聽遠處旁觀者說法。差異不一定是謊言：每個人看到的角度不同，承認不確定時所要承擔的風險也不同。午夜後警局走廊聲音平凡。秦墨發現調查強大宣稱時最難的是給平凡疑問足夠時間。修訂檔案出現「進入；通行」一詞，秦墨拿原始頁面核對它的意思。沈嵐注意到初次檢查後有人在筆記裡加上「熟人」一詞。

**EN 02**

He handed Qin a letter naming the person who had paid for the original forged confession. Qin Mo refused to pretend the previous decision had solved everything. The practical result was still there, useful but narrow. For each thing the team knew, there were two things it had not measured. Qin Mo drew a line through the claims that depended on speculation and asked Shen Lan to keep the original record intact. An official memo described the plan as aggressive, but Qin Mo refused to treat an adjective as evidence. The summary called the situation ashamed; Qin Mo asked which observation justified that description. One witness used the word competitive; Shen Lan noted that a description was not a measurement.

**繁中 02**

他交給秦墨一封信，寫明當年支付偽造供詞費用的人。秦墨不願假裝前一次決定已解決所有問題。成果確實存在，卻有明確界限。每個已知事項旁都還有未測量的部分。秦墨劃掉靠猜測成立的說法，請沈嵐保留原始紀錄。官方備忘錄形容計畫「咄咄逼人的」，秦墨不願把形容詞當作證據。摘要以「羞愧」形容情況，秦墨追問這種說法有何觀察根據。一位證人用了「競賽性的」一詞，沈嵐指出形容並不等於測量。

**EN 03**

A small disagreement changed the schedule. Qin Mo wanted to proceed before evidence disappeared; Shen Lan wanted another check because a wrong conclusion might hurt people who could not easily defend themselves. They did not settle the matter by choosing a leader. They wrote down what a short, reversible test could actually establish. Shen Lan noticed the word council in a note that someone had added after the first inspection. A handwritten label read criticism, but no one could say who had written it there. The term economy appeared in the revised file; Qin Mo checked its meaning against the original page.

**繁中 03**

一場小爭執改變了時程。秦墨想趁證據消失前行動；沈嵐要求再核對一次，因為錯誤結論可能傷害難以自辯的人。他們沒有靠選領袖解決爭議，而是列出短暫、可逆測試真正能證明什麼。沈嵐注意到初次檢查後有人在筆記裡加上「議會」一詞。手寫標籤寫著「批評」，卻沒有人知道是誰留下的。修訂檔案出現「經濟」一詞，秦墨拿原始頁面核對它的意思。

**EN 04**

The policy supplied motive but not opportunity, forcing investigators to keep the insurance theory separate from proof. The plan had been amended three times, and each change left a trace. Qin Mo valued those corrections more than a perfect-looking first draft. If the outcome proved disappointing, the group would still be able to identify which assumption needed repair. Shen Lan watched for the quiet participant who had not yet been invited to speak. The term exhibit appeared in the revised file; Qin Mo checked its meaning against the original page. Shen Lan noticed the word formula in a note that someone had added after the first inspection. Shen Lan repeated the instruction to hasten, then asked whether anyone had the right to refuse.

**繁中 04**

保單提供動機，不提供行兇機會，調查者必須分清假設與證據。計畫改了三次，每次修改都留下痕跡。秦墨認為這些修正比看似完美的初稿更有價值。就算結果不理想，團隊仍能知道該修正哪一項假設。沈嵐也注意到那名一直沒有發言機會的人。修訂檔案出現「證物」一詞，秦墨拿原始頁面核對它的意思。沈嵐注意到初次檢查後有人在筆記裡加上「公式」一詞。沈嵐重述「加速」的指示，再問當事人是否有權拒絕。

**EN 05**

An official version reached the room before the practical work was finished. Qin Mo read it once, then compared it with what they had actually measured. The difference was not always malicious, but it could still hurt somebody. Shen Lan asked that the correction be written in language the affected people could understand. The station corridor sounded ordinary after midnight. Qin Mo found that the hardest part of investigating a powerful claim was giving an ordinary doubt enough time. Shen Lan repeated the instruction to immigrate, then asked whether anyone had the right to refuse. The summary called the situation keen; Qin Mo asked which observation justified that description. On the next form, the action field read moreover, but the witness had never approved the operation.

**EN 07**

一場小爭執改變了時程。秦墨想趁證據消失前行動；沈嵐要求再核對一次，因為錯誤結論可能傷害難以自辯的人。他們沒有靠選領袖解決爭議，而是列出短暫、可逆測試真正能證明什麼。修訂檔案出現「懸崖」一詞，秦墨拿原始頁面核對它的意思。沈嵐注意到初次檢查後有人在筆記裡加上「勤勉」一詞。沈嵐重述「干擾」的指示，再問當事人是否有權拒絕。

**EN 04**

Shen Lan found fresh adhesive on an audio switch that linked the hearing to the murder studio. The plan had been amended three times, and each change left a trace. Qin Mo valued those corrections more than a perfect-looking first draft. If the outcome proved disappointing, the group would still be able to identify which assumption needed repair. Shen Lan watched for the quiet participant who had not yet been invited to speak. A handwritten label read instructor, but no one could say who had written it there. The term makeup appeared in the revised file; Qin Mo checked its meaning against the original page. On the next form, the action field read nowadays, but the witness had never approved the operation.

**繁中 04**

沈嵐在音訊切換器上發現新鮮黏膠，設備連到命案攝影棚。計畫改了三次，每次修改都留下痕跡。秦墨認為這些修正比看似完美的初稿更有價值。就算結果不理想，團隊仍能知道該修正哪一項假設。沈嵐也注意到那名一直沒有發言機會的人。手寫標籤寫著「教練」，卻沒有人知道是誰留下的。修訂檔案出現「化妝；舞臺妝」一詞，秦墨拿原始頁面核對它的意思。下一張表單把動作寫成「如今」，見證人卻從未同意這項操作。

**EN 05**

An official version reached the room before the practical work was finished. Qin Mo read it once, then compared it with what they had actually measured. The difference was not always malicious, but it could still hurt somebody. Shen Lan asked that the correction be written in language the affected people could understand. The interview recorder captured words but not the years of rehearsal behind them. Shen Lan insisted on laboratory tests before a dramatic confession became a charge. Shen Lan noticed the word pronunciation in a note that someone had added after the first inspection. People disagreed about publicity; Qin Mo separated the feeling from the facts they could test. The instructions used the verb regulate without naming who would carry the risk; Qin Mo circled that gap.

**繁中 05**

實際工作還沒完成，官方版本就傳到現場。秦墨讀了一次，再比對真正測得的結果。差異未必都是惡意，卻仍可能傷害人。沈嵐要求把更正寫成受影響者看得懂的語言。偵訊錄音留下話語，留不下背後多年的排練。沈嵐堅持在戲劇性認罪變成指控前先完成實驗室檢驗。沈嵐注意到初次檢查後有人在筆記裡加上「發音」一詞。大家對「宣傳」意見不同，秦墨把感受與可檢驗的事實分開。指示裡用了「調節；控制」這個動詞，卻沒說誰承擔風險；秦墨圈出這項缺漏。

**EN 06**

A witness asked whether the team was taking sides. Qin Mo answered that refusing to invent a fact was not the same as refusing to care. What mattered was whether the procedure protected someone whose story was less convenient to tell. Shen Lan listened without interrupting and wrote down the question exactly as it had been asked. The term retreat appeared in the revised file; Qin Mo checked its meaning against the original page. Shen Lan noticed the word scratch in a note that someone had added after the first inspection. A handwritten label read socket, but no one could say who had written it there.

**繁中 06**

一名見證人問團隊是否偏袒。秦墨回答，不捏造事實不代表冷漠。真正重要的是程序能否保護那個故事不方便被說出口的人。沈嵐沒有打斷，逐字記下原問題。修訂檔案出現「撤離」一詞，秦墨拿原始頁面核對它的意思。沈嵐注意到初次檢查後有人在筆記裡加上「沙沙雜音」一詞。手寫標籤寫著「插座」，卻沒有人知道是誰留下的。

**EN 07**

Even after the group agreed on the immediate step, another question remained. The answer would not arrive through a dramatic announcement. Someone would have to return to the place, compare the earlier notes, and accept the possibility that the next piece of evidence might spoil an attractive theory. Qin Mo prepared to do exactly that. The station corridor sounded ordinary after midnight. Qin Mo found that the hardest part of investigating a powerful claim was giving an ordinary doubt enough time. A handwritten label read submarine, but no one could say who had written it there. The summary called the situation technological; Qin Mo asked which observation justified that description. Shen Lan noticed the word transportation in a note that someone had added after the first inspection.

**繁中 07**

即使大家同意眼前步驟，另一個問題仍在。答案不會靠戲劇性公告降臨；有人得返回現場、比對舊筆記，並接受新證據可能推翻漂亮理論。秦墨準備做的正是這件事。午夜後警局走廊聲音平凡。秦墨發現調查強大宣稱時最難的是給平凡疑問足夠時間。手寫標籤寫著「潛水艇」，卻沒有人知道是誰留下的。摘要以「技術的」形容情況，秦墨追問這種說法有何觀察根據。沈嵐注意到初次檢查後有人在筆記裡加上「交通」一詞。

## CHAPTER 16｜The Hidden Director｜看不見的導演

### 正文與翻譯

**EN 01**

Investigators traced the switch to a private director who had coached the prime suspect and timed the edited recordings. Something had changed, but not everybody noticed it in the same order. Qin Mo listened to the account of the nearest worker, then to someone standing farther away. Their differences were not necessarily lies. Each person had been given a different view and a different reason to be afraid of admitting uncertainty. The station corridor sounded ordinary after midnight. Qin Mo found that the hardest part of investigating a powerful claim was giving an ordinary doubt enough time. Shen Lan noticed the word addict in a note that someone had added after the first inspection. A handwritten label read aspect, but no one could say who had written it there.

**繁中 01**

調查人員追到一名私下導演，他指導頭號嫌疑人並安排經剪輯的錄音時間。某件事改變了，但人們發現它的順序不同。秦墨先聽近處工作人員的描述，再聽遠處旁觀者說法。差異不一定是謊言：每個人看到的角度不同，承認不確定時所要承擔的風險也不同。午夜後警局走廊聲音平凡。秦墨發現調查強大宣稱時最難的是給平凡疑問足夠時間。沈嵐注意到初次檢查後有人在筆記裡加上「沉迷者」一詞。手寫標籤寫著「面向」，卻沒有人知道是誰留下的。

**EN 02**

Shen Lan found fresh adhesive on an audio switch that linked the hearing to the murder studio. Qin Mo refused to pretend the previous decision had solved everything. The practical result was still there, useful but narrow. For each thing the team knew, there were two things it had not measured. Qin Mo drew a line through the claims that depended on speculation and asked Shen Lan to keep the original record intact. The term celebration appeared in the revised file; Qin Mo checked its meaning against the original page. Shen Lan noticed the word container in a note that someone had added after the first inspection. Shen Lan repeated the instruction to distinguish, then asked whether anyone had the right to refuse.

**繁中 02**

沈嵐在音訊切換器上發現新鮮黏膠，設備連到命案攝影棚。秦墨不願假裝前一次決定已解決所有問題。成果確實存在，卻有明確界限。每個已知事項旁都還有未測量的部分。秦墨劃掉靠猜測成立的說法，請沈嵐保留原始紀錄。修訂檔案出現「慶祝」一詞，秦墨拿原始頁面核對它的意思。沈嵐注意到初次檢查後有人在筆記裡加上「容器」一詞。沈嵐重述「區分」的指示，再問當事人是否有權拒絕。

**EN 03**

A small disagreement changed the schedule. Qin Mo wanted to proceed before evidence disappeared; Shen Lan wanted another check because a wrong conclusion might hurt people who could not easily defend themselves. They did not settle the matter by choosing a leader. They wrote down what a short, reversible test could actually establish. The earlier photograph clearly showed a flea; the newer version did not, so Qin Mo kept both. A note about the gallery lay beside the old register, with a date that Qin Mo could not verify. Shen Lan pointed to the hive in the inventory and asked why its location had changed.

**繁中 03**

一場小爭執改變了時程。秦墨想趁證據消失前行動；沈嵐要求再核對一次，因為錯誤結論可能傷害難以自辯的人。他們沒有靠選領袖解決爭議，而是列出短暫、可逆測試真正能證明什麼。較早的照片清楚顯示一項「跳蚤」，新版卻沒有，秦墨因此保留兩張。一張談到「展覽廳」的便條放在舊登記簿旁，日期讓秦墨無法核實。沈嵐指向清單上的「蜂巢」，追問其位置為何改變。

The director admitted staging the accusation but denied the killings, giving the team a testable contradiction. The plan had been amended three times, and each change left a trace. Qin Mo valued those corrections more than a perfect-looking first draft. If the outcome proved disappointing, the group would still be able to identify which assumption needed repair. Shen Lan watched for the quiet participant who had not yet been invited to speak. One witness used the word intensive; Shen Lan noted that a description was not a measurement. Shen Lan repeated the instruction to kneel, then asked whether anyone had the right to refuse. The term knob appeared in the revised file; Qin Mo checked its meaning against the original page.

導演承認安排指控場面，否認行兇，留下可檢驗的矛盾。計畫改了三次，每次修改都留下痕跡。秦墨認為這些修正比看似完美的初稿更有價值。就算結果不理想，團隊仍能知道該修正哪一項假設。沈嵐也注意到那名一直沒有發言機會的人。一位證人用了「密集的」一詞，沈嵐指出形容並不等於測量。沈嵐重述「跪下」的指示，再問當事人是否有權拒絕。修訂檔案出現「旋鈕」一詞，秦墨拿原始頁面核對它的意思。

A witness asked whether the team was taking sides. Qin Mo answered that refusing to invent a fact was not the same as refusing to care.
