**EN 07**

Even after the group agreed on the immediate step, another question remained. The answer would not arrive through a dramatic announcement. Someone would have to return to the place, compare the earlier notes, and accept the possibility that the next piece of evidence might spoil an attractive theory. Qin Mo prepared to do exactly that. The interview recorder captured words but not the years of rehearsal behind them. Shen Lan insisted on laboratory tests before a dramatic confession became a charge. Shen Lan noticed the word satisfaction in a note that someone had added after the first inspection. A handwritten label read sprinkle, but no one could say who had written it there. ## CHAPTER 17｜Two Doors and One Witness｜兩扇門與一位證人

### 正文與翻譯

**EN 01**

A cleaner recalled seeing the producer leave through a service door before security cameras recorded his arrival at the main entrance. Something had changed, but not everybody noticed it in the same order. Qin Mo listened to the account of the nearest worker, then to someone standing farther away. Their differences were not necessarily lies. Each person had been given a different view and a different reason to be afraid of admitting uncertainty. The interview recorder captured words but not the years of rehearsal behind them. Shen Lan insisted on laboratory tests before a dramatic confession became a charge. The summary called the situation adequate; Qin Mo asked which observation justified that description. One witness used the word agreeable; Shen Lan noted that a description was not a measurement.

**繁中 01**

清潔員記得製作人在監視器拍到他走進正門前，已從側門離開。某件事改變了，但人們發現它的順序不同。秦墨先聽近處工作人員的描述，再聽遠處旁觀者說法。差異不一定是謊言：每個人看到的角度不同，承認不確定時所要承擔的風險也不同。偵訊錄音留下話語，留不下背後多年的排練。沈嵐堅持在戲劇性認罪變成指控前先完成實驗室檢驗。摘要以「足夠的」形容情況，秦墨追問這種說法有何觀察根據。一位證人用了「令人接受的」一詞，沈嵐指出形容並不等於測量。

**EN 02**

The director admitted staging the accusation but denied the killings, giving the team a testable contradiction. Qin Mo refused to pretend the previous decision had solved everything. The practical result was still there, useful but narrow. For each thing the team knew, there were two things it had not measured. Qin Mo drew a line through the claims that depended on speculation and asked Shen Lan to keep the original record intact. A handwritten label read assistance, but no one could say who had written it there. The instructions used the verb demonstrate without naming who would carry the risk; Qin Mo circled that gap. On the next form, the action field read elsewhere, but the witness had never approved the operation.

**繁中 02**

導演承認安排指控場面，否認行兇，留下可檢驗的矛盾。秦墨不願假裝前一次決定已解決所有問題。成果確實存在，卻有明確界限。每個已知事項旁都還有未測量的部分。秦墨劃掉靠猜測成立的說法，請沈嵐保留原始紀錄。手寫標籤寫著「協助」，卻沒有人知道是誰留下的。指示裡用了「示範；證明」這個動詞，卻沒說誰承擔風險；秦墨圈出這項缺漏。下一張表單把動作寫成「在其他地方」，見證人卻從未同意這項操作。

**EN 03**

A small disagreement changed the schedule. Qin Mo wanted to proceed before evidence disappeared; Shen Lan wanted another check because a wrong conclusion might hurt people who could not easily defend themselves. They did not settle the matter by choosing a leader. They wrote down what a short, reversible test could actually establish. Shen Lan pointed to the fax in the inventory and asked why its location had changed. The earlier photograph clearly showed a lipstick; the newer version did not, so Qin Mo kept both. The term maturity appeared in the revised file; Qin Mo checked its meaning against the original page.

**繁中 03**

一場小爭執改變了時程。秦墨想趁證據消失前行動；沈嵐要求再核對一次，因為錯誤結論可能傷害難以自辯的人。他們沒有靠選領袖解決爭議，而是列出短暫、可逆測試真正能證明什麼。沈嵐指向清單上的「傳真機」，追問其位置為何改變。較早的照片清楚顯示一項「口紅」，新版卻沒有，秦墨因此保留兩張。修訂檔案出現「成熟」一詞，秦墨拿原始頁面核對它的意思。

**EN 04**

The floor plan showed a hidden route used by both victims' visitors, challenging the apparent alibis. The plan had been amended three times, and each change left a trace. Qin Mo valued those corrections more than a perfect-looking first draft. If the outcome proved disappointing, the group would still be able to identify which assumption needed repair. Shen Lan watched for the quiet participant who had not yet been invited to speak. A note about the memorial lay beside the old register, with a date that Qin Mo could not verify. On the next form, the action field read overcome, but the witness had never approved the operation. A handwritten label read polish, but no one could say who had written it there.

**繁中 04**

平面圖顯示兩名死者訪客都用過的隱藏動線，推翻表面的不在場證明。計畫改了三次，每次修改都留下痕跡。秦墨認為這些修正比看似完美的初稿更有價值。就算結果不理想，團隊仍能知道該修正哪一項假設。沈嵐也注意到那名一直沒有發言機會的人。一張談到「紀念牆」的便條放在舊登記簿旁，日期讓秦墨無法核實。下一張表單把動作寫成「克服」，見證人卻從未同意這項操作。手寫標籤寫著「潤飾」，卻沒有人知道是誰留下的。

**EN 05**

An official version reached the room before the practical work was finished. Qin Mo read it once, then compared it with what they had actually measured. The difference was not always malicious, but it could still hurt somebody. Shen Lan asked that the correction be written in language the affected people could understand. The interview recorder captured words but not the years of rehearsal behind them. Shen Lan insisted on laboratory tests before a dramatic confession became a charge. A handwritten label read quarrel, but no one could say who had written it there. The summary called the situation reluctant; Qin Mo asked which observation justified that description. Shen Lan noticed the word repetition in a note that someone had added after the first inspection.

**繁中 05**

實際工作還沒完成，官方版本就傳到現場。秦墨讀了一次，再比對真正測得的結果。差異未必都是惡意，卻仍可能傷害人。沈嵐要求把更正寫成受影響者看得懂的語言。偵訊錄音留下話語，留不下背後多年的排練。沈嵐堅持在戲劇性認罪變成指控前先完成實驗室檢驗。手寫標籤寫著「爭吵」，卻沒有人知道是誰留下的。摘要以「不情願的」形容情況，秦墨追問這種說法有何觀察根據。沈嵐注意到初次檢查後有人在筆記裡加上「重複」一詞。

**EN 06**

A witness asked whether the team was taking sides. Qin Mo answered that refusing to invent a fact was not the same as refusing to care. What mattered was whether the procedure protected someone whose story was less convenient to tell. Shen Lan listened without interrupting and wrote down the question exactly as it had been asked. On the next form, the action field read resign, but the witness had never approved the operation. A handwritten label read reunion, but no one could say who had written it there. The term sacrifice appeared in the revised file; Qin Mo checked its meaning against the original page.

**繁中 06**

一名見證人問團隊是否偏袒。秦墨回答，不捏造事實不代表冷漠。真正重要的是程序能否保護那個故事不方便被說出口的人。沈嵐沒有打斷，逐字記下原問題。下一張表單把動作寫成「辭職」，見證人卻從未同意這項操作。手寫標籤寫著「聚會」，卻沒有人知道是誰留下的。修訂檔案出現「犧牲」一詞，秦墨拿原始頁面核對它的意思。

**EN 07**

Even after the group agreed on the immediate step, another question remained. The answer would not arrive through a dramatic announcement. Someone would have to return to the place, compare the earlier notes, and accept the possibility that the next piece of evidence might spoil an attractive theory. Qin Mo prepared to do exactly that. The station corridor sounded ordinary after midnight. Qin Mo found that the hardest part of investigating a powerful claim was giving an ordinary doubt enough time. The term statistic appeared in the revised file; Qin Mo checked its meaning against the original page. Shen Lan noticed the word tragedy in a note that someone had added after the first inspection. A handwritten label read wit, but no one could say who had written it there.

**繁中 07**

即使大家同意眼前步驟，另一個問題仍在。答案不會靠戲劇性公告降臨；有人得返回現場、比對舊筆記，並接受新證據可能推翻漂亮理論。秦墨準備做的正是這件事。午夜後警局走廊聲音平凡。秦墨發現調查強大宣稱時最難的是給平凡疑問足夠時間。修訂檔案出現「統計數據」一詞，秦墨拿原始頁面核對它的意思。沈嵐注意到初次檢查後有人在筆記裡加上「悲劇」一詞。手寫標籤寫著「機智」，卻沒有人知道是誰留下的。

### 理解測驗

**Q1. Which development is established in Chapter 17?**

A. The investigators decide that every earlier warning was fictional.

B. A cleaner recalled seeing the producer leave through a service door before security cameras recorded his arrival at the main entrance.

C. The central problem disappears without further action.

**答案：B。** 清潔員記得製作人在監視器拍到他走進正門前，已從側門離開。

**Q2. What new complication or decision changes the investigation in this chapter?**

A. All the witnesses agree to destroy their records.

B. The team abandons the people affected and declares a perfect victory.

C. The floor plan showed a hidden route used by both victims' visitors, challenging the apparent alibis.

**答案：C。** 平面圖顯示兩名死者訪客都用過的隱藏動線，推翻表面的不在場證明。


## CHAPTER 18｜The Poisoned Script｜染毒的劇本

### 正文與翻譯

**EN 01**

Shen Lan identified a slow-acting toxin on an archive binder handled by both victims, with different exposure times matching their deaths. Something had changed, but not everybody noticed it in the same order. Qin Mo listened to the account of the nearest worker, then to someone standing farther away. Their differences were not necessarily lies. Each person had been given a different view and a different reason to be afraid of admitting uncertainty. The station corridor sounded ordinary after midnight. Qin Mo found that the hardest part of investigating a powerful claim was giving an ordinary doubt enough time. An official memo described the plan as admirable, but Qin Mo refused to treat an adjective as evidence. The term ballet appeared in the revised file; Qin Mo checked its meaning against the original page.

**繁中 01**

沈嵐在兩名死者都碰過的檔案夾發現慢性毒物，接觸時間符合各自死期。某件事改變了，但人們發現它的順序不同。秦墨先聽近處工作人員的描述，再聽遠處旁觀者說法。差異不一定是謊言：每個人看到的角度不同，承認不確定時所要承擔的風險也不同。午夜後警局走廊聲音平凡。秦墨發現調查強大宣稱時最難的是給平凡疑問足夠時間。官方備忘錄形容計畫「值得稱讚的」，秦墨不願把形容詞當作證據。修訂檔案出現「芭蕾」一詞，秦墨拿原始頁面核對它的意思。

The practical result was still there, useful but narrow. For each thing the team knew, there were two things it had not measured. Qin Mo drew a line through the claims that depended on speculation and asked Shen Lan to keep the original record intact. Shen Lan pointed to the battery in the inventory and asked why its location had changed. A handwritten label read chew, but no one could say who had written it there. The term colleague appeared in the revised file; Qin Mo checked its meaning against the original page.

### 理解測驗

**Q1. Which development is established in Chapter 18?**

A. The investigators decide that every earlier warning was fictional.

B. Shen Lan identified a slow-acting toxin on an archive binder handled by both victims, with different exposure times matching their deaths.

C. The central problem disappears without further action.

**答案：B。** 沈嵐在兩名死者都碰過的檔案夾發現慢性毒物，接觸時間符合各自死期。

**Q2. What new complication or decision changes the investigation in this chapter?**

A. All the witnesses agree to destroy their records.

B. The team abandons the people affected and declares a perfect victory.

C. Only one employee had ordered the binder replaced after the scandal, but the purchase order used a borrowed signature.

**答案：C。** 醜聞後只有一名員工訂了替換檔案夾，採購單卻用借來的簽名。


## CHAPTER 19｜The Face Behind the Words｜字句背後的臉

### 正文與翻譯

**EN 01**

The assistant's surviving sibling revealed that the assistant was about to expose the producer's payments for false testimony. Something had changed, but not everybody noticed it in the same order. Qin Mo listened to the account of the nearest worker, then to someone standing farther away. Their differences were not necessarily lies. Each person had been given a different view and a different reason to be afraid of admitting uncertainty. The interview recorder captured words but not the years of rehearsal behind them. Shen Lan insisted on laboratory tests before a dramatic confession became a charge. Shen Lan noticed the word applicant in a note that someone had added after the first inspection. A handwritten label read application, but no one could say who had written it there.

**繁中 01**

前助理的手足表示，助理原打算揭露製作人支付假證詞的款項。某件事改變了，但人們發現它的順序不同。秦墨先聽近處工作人員的描述，再聽遠處旁觀者說法。差異不一定是謊言：每個人看到的角度不同，承認不確定時所要承擔的風險也不同。偵訊錄音留下話語，留不下背後多年的排練。沈嵐堅持在戲劇性認罪變成指控前先完成實驗室檢驗。沈嵐注意到初次檢查後有人在筆記裡加上「申請人」一詞。手寫標籤寫著「申請」，卻沒有人知道是誰留下的。

**EN 02**

Only one employee had ordered the binder replaced after the scandal, but the purchase order used a borrowed signature. Qin Mo refused to pretend the previous decision had solved everything. The practical result was still there, useful but narrow. For each thing the team knew, there were two things it had not measured. Qin Mo drew a line through the claims that depended on speculation and asked Shen Lan to keep the original record intact. The term classification appeared in the revised file; Qin Mo checked its meaning against the original page. Shen Lan noticed the word comedy in a note that someone had added after the first inspection. A handwritten label read critic, but no one could say who had written it there.

**繁中 02**

醜聞後只有一名員工訂了替換檔案夾，採購單卻用借來的簽名。秦墨不願假裝前一次決定已解決所有問題。成果確實存在，卻有明確界限。每個已知事項旁都還有未測量的部分。秦墨劃掉靠猜測成立的說法，請沈嵐保留原始紀錄。修訂檔案出現「分類」一詞，秦墨拿原始頁面核對它的意思。沈嵐注意到初次檢查後有人在筆記裡加上「喜劇」一詞。手寫標籤寫著「評論者」，卻沒有人知道是誰留下的。

**EN 03**

A small disagreement changed the schedule. Qin Mo wanted to proceed before evidence disappeared; Shen Lan wanted another check because a wrong conclusion might hurt people who could not easily defend themselves. They did not settle the matter by choosing a leader. They wrote down what a short, reversible test could actually establish. An official memo described the plan as digital, but Qin Mo refused to treat an adjective as evidence. The term fiction appeared in the revised file; Qin Mo checked its meaning against the original page. Shen Lan noticed the word flush in a note that someone had added after the first inspection.

**繁中 03**

一場小爭執改變了時程。秦墨想趁證據消失前行動；沈嵐要求再核對一次，因為錯誤結論可能傷害難以自辯的人。他們沒有靠選領袖解決爭議，而是列出短暫、可逆測試真正能證明什麼。官方備忘錄形容計畫「數位的」，秦墨不願把形容詞當作證據。修訂檔案出現「虛構故事」一詞，秦墨拿原始頁面核對它的意思。沈嵐注意到初次檢查後有人在筆記裡加上「清空」一詞。

**EN 04**

Qin realized the first victim had threatened the producer, while the second carried the documents that could prove it. The plan had been amended three times, and each change left a trace. Qin Mo valued those corrections more than a perfect-looking first draft. If the outcome proved disappointing, the group would still be able to identify which assumption needed repair. Shen Lan watched for the quiet participant who had not yet been invited to speak. One witness used the word gifted; Shen Lan noted that a description was not a measurement. A handwritten label read investigation, but no one could say who had written it there. The term lecture appeared in the revised file; Qin Mo checked its meaning against the original page.

**繁中 04**

秦墨明白首名死者威脅了製作人，第二名死者則持有能證明這件事的文件。計畫改了三次，每次修改都留下痕跡。秦墨認為這些修正比看似完美的初稿更有價值。就算結果不理想，團隊仍能知道該修正哪一項假設。沈嵐也注意到那名一直沒有發言機會的人。一位證人用了「有天賦的」一詞，沈嵐指出形容並不等於測量。手寫標籤寫著「調查」，卻沒有人知道是誰留下的。修訂檔案出現「講座」一詞，秦墨拿原始頁面核對它的意思。

**EN 05**

An official version reached the room before the practical work was finished. Qin Mo read it once, then compared it with what they had actually measured. The difference was not always malicious, but it could still hurt somebody. Shen Lan asked that the correction be written in language the affected people could understand. The interview recorder captured words but not the years of rehearsal behind them. Shen Lan insisted on laboratory tests before a dramatic confession became a charge. The summary called the situation logical; Qin Mo asked which observation justified that description. Shen Lan noticed the word manufacturer in a note that someone had added after the first inspection. An official memo described the plan as occasional, but Qin Mo refused to treat an adjective as evidence.

**繁中 05**

實際工作還沒完成，官方版本就傳到現場。秦墨讀了一次，再比對真正測得的結果。差異未必都是惡意，卻仍可能傷害人。沈嵐要求把更正寫成受影響者看得懂的語言。偵訊錄音留下話語，留不下背後多年的排練。沈嵐堅持在戲劇性認罪變成指控前先完成實驗室檢驗。摘要以「合乎邏輯的」形容情況，秦墨追問這種說法有何觀察根據。沈嵐注意到初次檢查後有人在筆記裡加上「製造商」一詞。官方備忘錄形容計畫「偶爾的」，秦墨不願把形容詞當作證據。

**EN 06**

A witness asked whether the team was taking sides. Qin Mo answered that refusing to invent a fact was not the same as refusing to care. What mattered was whether the procedure protected someone whose story was less convenient to tell. Shen Lan listened without interrupting and wrote down the question exactly as it had been asked. Shen Lan repeated the instruction to otherwise, then asked whether anyone had the right to refuse. The report claimed philosophy, but Qin Mo wanted an example instead of a reassuring noun. Shen Lan noticed the word physician in a note that someone had added after the first inspection.

**繁中 06**

一名見證人問團隊是否偏袒。秦墨回答，不捏造事實不代表冷漠。真正重要的是程序能否保護那個故事不方便被說出口的人。沈嵐沒有打斷，逐字記下原問題。沈嵐重述「否則」的指示，再問當事人是否有權拒絕。報告聲稱有「哲學」，秦墨要求具體例子，而不是安撫人的名詞。沈嵐注意到初次檢查後有人在筆記裡加上「醫師」一詞。

**EN 07**

Even after the group agreed on the immediate step, another question remained. The answer would not arrive through a dramatic announcement. Someone would have to return to the place, compare the earlier notes, and accept the possibility that the next piece of evidence might spoil an attractive theory. Qin Mo prepared to do exactly that. The station corridor sounded ordinary after midnight. Qin Mo found that the hardest part of investigating a powerful claim was giving an ordinary doubt enough time. Shen Lan noticed the word recall in a note that someone had added after the first inspection. Shen Lan repeated the instruction to resemble, then asked whether anyone had the right to refuse. The term reservation appeared in the revised file; Qin Mo checked its meaning against the original page.

**繁中 07**

即使大家同意眼前步驟，另一個問題仍在。答案不會靠戲劇性公告降臨；有人得返回現場、比對舊筆記，並接受新證據可能推翻漂亮理論。秦墨準備做的正是這件事。午夜後警局走廊聲音平凡。秦墨發現調查強大宣稱時最難的是給平凡疑問足夠時間。沈嵐注意到初次檢查後有人在筆記裡加上「記起」一詞。沈嵐重述「很相似」的指示，再問當事人是否有權拒絕。修訂檔案出現「預約」一詞，秦墨拿原始頁面核對它的意思。

即使大家同意眼前步驟，另一個問題仍在。答案不會靠戲劇性公告降臨；有人得返回現場、比對舊筆記，並接受新證據可能推翻漂亮理論。秦墨準備做的正是這件事。偵訊錄音留下話語，留不下背後多年的排練。沈嵐堅持在戲劇性認罪變成指控前先完成實驗室檢驗。報告聲稱有「拒絕」，秦墨要求具體例子，而不是安撫人的名詞。一位證人用了「緊繃的」一詞，沈嵐指出形容並不等於測量。沈嵐重述「退出；撤回」的指示，再問當事人是否有權拒絕。

**Q1. Which development is established in Chapter 20?**

A. The investigators decide that every earlier warning was fictional.

B. Qin confronted the producer with purchase records, timelines, and toxin evidence rather than telling him what the floating sentence predicted.

C. The central problem disappears without further action.

**答案：B。** 秦墨用採購紀錄、時間表及毒物證據質問製作人，沒有透露頭上的預言。

**Q2. What new complication or decision changes the investigation in this chapter?**

A. All the witnesses agree to destroy their records.

B. The team abandons the people affected and declares a perfect victory.

C. The producer's visible final line was again THE KILLER IS YOU, but he looked directly at a mirror behind Qin.

**答案：C。** 製作人頭上的最後一句又是「兇手是你」，目光卻看向秦墨身後的鏡子。


## CHAPTER 21｜The Mirror Test｜鏡子測試

### 正文與翻譯

**EN 01**

Shen Lan recreated the interview with two mirrors and a neutral speaker. The visible line followed the person's intended listener, not an objective target. Something had changed, but not everybody noticed it in the same order. Qin Mo listened to the account of the nearest worker, then to someone standing farther away. Their differences were not necessarily lies. Each person had been given a different view and a different reason to be afraid of admitting uncertainty. The interview recorder captured words but not the years of rehearsal behind them. Shen Lan insisted on laboratory tests before a dramatic confession became a charge. A handwritten label read arms, but no one could say who had written it there. The term bloom appeared in the revised file; Qin Mo checked its meaning against the original page.

**繁中 01**

沈嵐用兩面鏡子與中立發言者重建問話，文字指向對方打算說話的聽眾，不是客觀罪犯。某件事改變了，但人們發現它的順序不同。秦墨先聽近處工作人員的描述，再聽遠處旁觀者說法。差異不一定是謊言：每個人看到的角度不同，承認不確定時所要承擔的風險也不同。偵訊錄音留下話語，留不下背後多年的排練。沈嵐堅持在戲劇性認罪變成指控前先完成實驗室檢驗。手寫標籤寫著「手臂」，卻沒有人知道是誰留下的。修訂檔案出現「綻放」一詞，秦墨拿原始頁面核對它的意思。

**EN 02**

The producer's visible final line was again THE KILLER IS YOU, but he looked directly at a mirror behind Qin. Qin Mo refused to pretend the previous decision had solved everything. The practical result was still there, useful but narrow. For each thing the team knew, there were two things it had not measured. Qin Mo drew a line through the claims that depended on speculation and asked Shen Lan to keep the original record intact. Shen Lan noticed the word concentration in a note that someone had added after the first inspection. Shen Lan repeated the instruction to consist, then asked whether anyone had the right to refuse. The term creativity appeared in the revised file; Qin Mo checked its meaning against the original page.

**繁中 02**

製作人頭上的最後一句又是「兇手是你」，目光卻看向秦墨身後的鏡子。秦墨不願假裝前一次決定已解決所有問題。成果確實存在，卻有明確界限。每個已知事項旁都還有未測量的部分。秦墨劃掉靠猜測成立的說法，請沈嵐保留原始紀錄。沈嵐注意到初次檢查後有人在筆記裡加上「集中」一詞。沈嵐重述「由……組成」的指示，再問當事人是否有權拒絕。修訂檔案出現「創意」一詞，秦墨拿原始頁面核對它的意思。

**EN 03**

A small disagreement changed the schedule. Qin Mo wanted to proceed before evidence disappeared; Shen Lan wanted another check because a wrong conclusion might hurt people who could not easily defend themselves. They did not settle the matter by choosing a leader. They wrote down what a short, reversible test could actually establish. The term disgust appeared in the revised file; Qin Mo checked its meaning against the original page. One witness used the word dynamic; Shen Lan noted that a description was not a measurement. Shen Lan repeated the instruction to exaggerate, then asked whether anyone had the right to refuse.

**繁中 03**

一場小爭執改變了時程。秦墨想趁證據消失前行動；沈嵐要求再核對一次，因為錯誤結論可能傷害難以自辯的人。他們沒有靠選領袖解決爭議，而是列出短暫、可逆測試真正能證明什麼。修訂檔案出現「厭惡」一詞，秦墨拿原始頁面核對它的意思。一位證人用了「動態的」一詞，沈嵐指出形容並不等於測量。沈嵐重述「誇大」的指示，再問當事人是否有權拒絕。

**EN 04**

The producer had rehearsed accusing Qin of covering up the old false conviction, hoping to derail the new murder case. The plan had been amended three times, and each change left a trace. Qin Mo valued those corrections more than a perfect-looking first draft. If the outcome proved disappointing, the group would still be able to identify which assumption needed repair. Shen Lan watched for the quiet participant who had not yet been invited to speak. A handwritten label read farewell, but no one could say who had written it there. The summary called the situation idle; Qin Mo asked which observation justified that description. On the next form, the action field read imitate, but the witness had never approved the operation.

**繁中 04**

製作人預演指控秦墨掩蓋舊冤案，想藉此轉移新命案的注意。計畫改了三次，每次修改都留下痕跡。秦墨認為這些修正比看似完美的初稿更有價值。就算結果不理想，團隊仍能知道該修正哪一項假設。沈嵐也注意到那名一直沒有發言機會的人。手寫標籤寫著「告別」，卻沒有人知道是誰留下的。摘要以「閒置的」形容情況，秦墨追問這種說法有何觀察根據。下一張表單把動作寫成「模仿」，見證人卻從未同意這項操作。

**EN 05**

An official version reached the room before the practical work was finished. Qin Mo read it once, then compared it with what they had actually measured. The difference was not always malicious, but it could still hurt somebody. Shen Lan asked that the correction be written in language the affected people could understand. The interview recorder captured words but not the years of rehearsal behind them. Shen Lan insisted on laboratory tests before a dramatic confession became a charge. On the next form, the action field read largely, but the witness had never approved the operation. A handwritten label read logic, but no one could say who had written it there. The summary called the situation numerous; Qin Mo asked which observation justified that description.

**繁中 05**

實際工作還沒完成，官方版本就傳到現場。秦墨讀了一次，再比對真正測得的結果。差異未必都是惡意，卻仍可能傷害人。沈嵐要求把更正寫成受影響者看得懂的語言。偵訊錄音留下話語，留不下背後多年的排練。沈嵐堅持在戲劇性認罪變成指控前先完成實驗室檢驗。下一張表單把動作寫成「大部分」，見證人卻從未同意這項操作。手寫標籤寫著「邏輯」，卻沒有人知道是誰留下的。摘要以「許多」形容情況，秦墨追問這種說法有何觀察根據。

**EN 06**

A witness asked whether the team was taking sides. Qin Mo answered that refusing to invent a fact was not the same as refusing to care. What mattered was whether the procedure protected someone whose story was less convenient to tell. Shen Lan listened without interrupting and wrote down the question exactly as it had been asked. The term objective appeared in the revised file; Qin Mo checked its meaning against the original page. Shen Lan noticed the word rejection in a note that someone had added after the first inspection. A handwritten label read remedy, but no one could say who had written it there.

**繁中 06**

一名見證人問團隊是否偏袒。秦墨回答，不捏造事實不代表冷漠。真正重要的是程序能否保護那個故事不方便被說出口的人。沈嵐沒有打斷，逐字記下原問題。修訂檔案出現「客觀的」一詞，秦墨拿原始頁面核對它的意思。沈嵐注意到初次檢查後有人在筆記裡加上「拒絕；排斥」一詞。手寫標籤寫著「補救方法」，卻沒有人知道是誰留下的。

**EN 07**

Even after the group agreed on the immediate step, another question remained. The answer would not arrive through a dramatic announcement. Someone would have to return to the place, compare the earlier notes, and accept the possibility that the next piece of evidence might spoil an attractive theory. Qin Mo prepared to do exactly that. The station corridor sounded ordinary after midnight. Qin Mo found that the hardest part of investigating a powerful claim was giving an ordinary doubt enough time. A handwritten label read singular, but no one could say who had written it there. The term vegetarian appeared in the revised file; Qin Mo checked its meaning against the original page. Shen Lan noticed the word workplace in a note that someone had added after the first inspection.

**繁中 07**

即使大家同意眼前步驟，另一個問題仍在。答案不會靠戲劇性公告降臨；有人得返回現場、比對舊筆記，並接受新證據可能推翻漂亮理論。秦墨準備做的正是這件事。午夜後警局走廊聲音平凡。秦墨發現調查強大宣稱時最難的是給平凡疑問足夠時間。手寫標籤寫著「單一的」，卻沒有人知道是誰留下的。修訂檔案出現「素食的」一詞，秦墨拿原始頁面核對它的意思。沈嵐注意到初次檢查後有人在筆記裡加上「工作場所」一詞。

實際工作還沒完成，官方版本就傳到現場。秦墨讀了一次，再比對真正測得的結果。差異未必都是惡意，卻仍可能傷害人。沈嵐要求把更正寫成受影響者看得懂的語言。午夜後警局走廊聲音平凡。秦墨發現調查強大宣稱時最難的是給平凡疑問足夠時間。修訂檔案出現「掛鉤」一詞，秦墨拿原始頁面核對它的意思。沈嵐注意到初次檢查後有人在筆記裡加上「隔離；遠離」一詞。官方備忘錄形容計畫「多個」，秦墨不願把形容詞當作證據。

**EN 06**

A witness asked whether the team was taking sides. Qin Mo answered that refusing to invent a fact was not the same as refusing to care. What mattered was whether the procedure protected someone whose story was less convenient to tell. Shen Lan listened without interrupting and wrote down the question exactly as it had been asked. An official memo described the plan as overnight, but Qin Mo refused to treat an adjective as evidence. The term quilt appeared in the revised file; Qin Mo checked its meaning against the original page. One witness used the word respectable; Shen Lan noted that a description was not a measurement.

**繁中 06**

一名見證人問團隊是否偏袒。秦墨回答，不捏造事實不代表冷漠。真正重要的是程序能否保護那個故事不方便被說出口的人。沈嵐沒有打斷，逐字記下原問題。官方備忘錄形容計畫「夜間的」，秦墨不願把形容詞當作證據。修訂檔案出現「棉被」一詞，秦墨拿原始頁面核對它的意思。一位證人用了「受人尊敬的」一詞，沈嵐指出形容並不等於測量。

**EN 07**

Even after the group agreed on the immediate step, another question remained. The answer would not arrive through a dramatic announcement. Someone would have to return to the place, compare the earlier notes, and accept the possibility that the next piece of evidence might spoil an attractive theory. Qin Mo prepared to do exactly that. The interview recorder captured words but not the years of rehearsal behind them. Shen Lan insisted on laboratory tests before a dramatic confession became a charge. One witness used the word secure; Shen Lan noted that a description was not a measurement. A handwritten label read spark, but no one could say who had written it there. The term stab appeared in the revised file; Qin Mo checked its meaning against the original page.

**繁中 07**

即使大家同意眼前步驟，另一個問題仍在。答案不會靠戲劇性公告降臨；有人得返回現場、比對舊筆記，並接受新證據可能推翻漂亮理論。秦墨準備做的正是這件事。偵訊錄音留下話語，留不下背後多年的排練。沈嵐堅持在戲劇性認罪變成指控前先完成實驗室檢驗。一位證人用了「安全的；保障」一詞，沈嵐指出形容並不等於測量。手寫標籤寫著「火花」，卻沒有人知道是誰留下的。修訂檔案出現「刺；刺擊」一詞，秦墨拿原始頁面核對它的意思。

### 理解測驗

**Q1. Which development is established in Chapter 22?**

A. The investigators decide that every earlier warning was fictional.

B. The team assembled independent evidence: service-door access, binder receipts, and chemical transfer from the producer's jacket.

C. The central problem disappears without further action.

**答案：B。** 團隊建立不依賴異能的證據鏈，包括側門紀錄、檔案夾收據及製作人外套上的化學轉移痕跡。

**Q2. What new complication or decision changes the investigation in this chapter?**

A. All the witnesses agree to destroy their records.

B. The team abandons the people affected and declares a perfect victory.

C. The prosecutor agreed the case could stand without Qin's supernatural claim, removing the main defense's attack.

**答案：C。** 檢察官認為案件不必依靠秦墨的超能力也能成立，避開主要辯護攻擊。


## CHAPTER 23｜Two Different Trials｜兩場不同的審判

### 正文與翻譯

**EN 01**

The murder investigation and the driver's wrongful-conviction review proceeded separately, so one result could not be traded against the other. Something had changed, but not everybody noticed it in the same order. Qin Mo listened to the account of the nearest worker, then to someone standing farther away. Their differences were not necessarily lies. Each person had been given a different view and a different reason to be afraid of admitting uncertainty. The interview recorder captured words but not the years of rehearsal behind them. Shen Lan insisted on laboratory tests before a dramatic confession became a charge. The summary called the situation artificial; Qin Mo asked which observation justified that description. On the next form, the action field read calculate, but the witness had never approved the operation.

**繁中 01**

謀殺調查與司機冤獄覆審分開進行，任何一方的結果都不能拿來交換另一方。某件事改變了，但人們發現它的順序不同。秦墨先聽近處工作人員的描述，再聽遠處旁觀者說法。差異不一定是謊言：每個人看到的角度不同，承認不確定時所要承擔的風險也不同。偵訊錄音留下話語，留不下背後多年的排練。沈嵐堅持在戲劇性認罪變成指控前先完成實驗室檢驗。摘要以「人工的」形容情況，秦墨追問這種說法有何觀察根據。下一張表單把動作寫成「計算」，見證人卻從未同意這項操作。

**EN 02**

The prosecutor agreed the case could stand without Qin's supernatural claim, removing the main defense's attack. Qin Mo refused to pretend the previous decision had solved everything. The practical result was still there, useful but narrow. For each thing the team knew, there were two things it had not measured. Qin Mo drew a line through the claims that depended on speculation and asked Shen Lan to keep the original record intact. A handwritten label read circulation, but no one could say who had written it there. The term contest appeared in the revised file; Qin Mo checked its meaning against the original page. On the next form, the action field read depart, but the witness had never approved the operation.

**繁中 02**

檢察官認為案件不必依靠秦墨的超能力也能成立，避開主要辯護攻擊。秦墨不願假裝前一次決定已解決所有問題。成果確實存在，卻有明確界限。每個已知事項旁都還有未測量的部分。秦墨劃掉靠猜測成立的說法，請沈嵐保留原始紀錄。手寫標籤寫著「人流動線」，卻沒有人知道是誰留下的。修訂檔案出現「競賽」一詞，秦墨拿原始頁面核對它的意思。下一張表單把動作寫成「離開」，見證人卻從未同意這項操作。

**EN 03**

A small disagreement changed the schedule. Qin Mo wanted to proceed before evidence disappeared; Shen Lan wanted another check because a wrong conclusion might hurt people who could not easily defend themselves. They did not settle the matter by choosing a leader. They wrote down what a short, reversible test could actually establish. Shen Lan noticed the word despite in a note that someone had added after the first inspection. A handwritten label read generosity, but no one could say who had written it there. The term incident appeared in the revised file; Qin Mo checked its meaning against the original page.

**繁中 03**

一場小爭執改變了時程。秦墨想趁證據消失前行動；沈嵐要求再核對一次，因為錯誤結論可能傷害難以自辯的人。他們沒有靠選領袖解決爭議，而是列出短暫、可逆測試真正能證明什麼。沈嵐注意到初次檢查後有人在筆記裡加上「儘管」一詞。手寫標籤寫著「慷慨」，卻沒有人知道是誰留下的。修訂檔案出現「事件」一詞，秦墨拿原始頁面核對它的意思。

**EN 04**

Qin publicly acknowledged his old mistake without claiming it made him responsible for the new murders. The plan had been amended three times, and each change left a trace. Qin Mo valued those corrections more than a perfect-looking first draft. If the outcome proved disappointing, the group would still be able to identify which assumption needed repair. Shen Lan watched for the quiet participant who had not yet been invited to speak. The term issue appeared in the revised file; Qin Mo checked its meaning against the original page. Shen Lan noticed the word laboratory in a note that someone had added after the first inspection. An official memo described the plan as modest, but Qin Mo refused to treat an adjective as evidence.

**繁中 04**

秦墨公開承認舊日錯誤，也不把這等同於自己犯下新命案。計畫改了三次，每次修改都留下痕跡。秦墨認為這些修正比看似完美的初稿更有價值。就算結果不理想，團隊仍能知道該修正哪一項假設。沈嵐也注意到那名一直沒有發言機會的人。修訂檔案出現「問題；議題」一詞，秦墨拿原始頁面核對它的意思。沈嵐注意到初次檢查後有人在筆記裡加上「實驗室」一詞。官方備忘錄形容計畫「樸素的」，秦墨不願把形容詞當作證據。

**EN 05**

An official version reached the room before the practical work was finished. Qin Mo read it once, then compared it with what they had actually measured. The difference was not always malicious, but it could still hurt somebody. Shen Lan asked that the correction be written in language the affected people could understand. The interview recorder captured words but not the years of rehearsal behind them. Shen Lan insisted on laboratory tests before a dramatic confession became a charge. A handwritten label read monument, but no one could say who had written it there. The summary called the situation muddy; Qin Mo asked which observation justified that description. Shen Lan noticed the word nursery in a note that someone had added after the first inspection.

**繁中 05**

實際工作還沒完成，官方版本就傳到現場。秦墨讀了一次，再比對真正測得的結果。差異未必都是惡意，卻仍可能傷害人。沈嵐要求把更正寫成受影響者看得懂的語言。偵訊錄音留下話語，留不下背後多年的排練。沈嵐堅持在戲劇性認罪變成指控前先完成實驗室檢驗。手寫標籤寫著「紀念碑」，卻沒有人知道是誰留下的。摘要以「泥濘的」形容情況，秦墨追問這種說法有何觀察根據。沈嵐注意到初次檢查後有人在筆記裡加上「托兒所」一詞。

**EN 06**

A witness asked whether the team was taking sides. Qin Mo answered that refusing to invent a fact was not the same as refusing to care. What mattered was whether the procedure protected someone whose story was less convenient to tell. Shen Lan listened without interrupting and wrote down the question exactly as it had been asked. Shen Lan noticed the word peer in a note that someone had added after the first inspection. An official memo described the plan as philosophical, but Qin Mo refused to treat an adjective as evidence. The instructions used the verb predict without naming who would carry the risk; Qin Mo circled that gap.

**繁中 06**

一名見證人問團隊是否偏袒。秦墨回答，不捏造事實不代表冷漠。真正重要的是程序能否保護那個故事不方便被說出口的人。沈嵐沒有打斷，逐字記下原問題。沈嵐注意到初次檢查後有人在筆記裡加上「同儕」一詞。官方備忘錄形容計畫「哲學上的」，秦墨不願把形容詞當作證據。指示裡用了「預測」這個動詞，卻沒說誰承擔風險；秦墨圈出這項缺漏。

**EN 07**

Even after the group agreed on the immediate step, another question remained. The answer would not arrive through a dramatic announcement. Someone would have to return to the place, compare the earlier notes, and accept the possibility that the next piece of evidence might spoil an attractive theory. Qin Mo prepared to do exactly that. The station corridor sounded ordinary after midnight. Qin Mo found that the hardest part of investigating a powerful claim was giving an ordinary doubt enough time. The term radar appeared in the revised file; Qin Mo checked its meaning against the original page. Shen Lan noticed the word scoop in a note that someone had added after the first inspection. A handwritten label read stem, but no one could say who had written it there.

**繁中 07**

即使大家同意眼前步驟，另一個問題仍在。答案不會靠戲劇性公告降臨；有人得返回現場、比對舊筆記，並接受新證據可能推翻漂亮理論。秦墨準備做的正是這件事。午夜後警局走廊聲音平凡。秦墨發現調查強大宣稱時最難的是給平凡疑問足夠時間。修訂檔案出現「雷達」一詞，秦墨拿原始頁面核對它的意思。沈嵐注意到初次檢查後有人在筆記裡加上「小鏟子」一詞。手寫標籤寫著「源於」，卻沒有人知道是誰留下的。

一場小爭執改變了時程。秦墨想趁證據消失前行動；沈嵐要求再核對一次，因為錯誤結論可能傷害難以自辯的人。他們沒有靠選領袖解決爭議，而是列出短暫、可逆測試真正能證明什麼。修訂檔案出現「章程」一詞，秦墨拿原始頁面核對它的意思。一位證人用了「傳統的」一詞，沈嵐指出形容並不等於測量。手寫標籤寫著「凹陷處」，卻沒有人知道是誰留下的。

**EN 04**

For the first time he entered an interview room without searching the air above anyone's head. The plan had been amended three times, and each change left a trace. Qin Mo valued those corrections more than a perfect-looking first draft. If the outcome proved disappointing, the group would still be able to identify which assumption needed repair. Shen Lan watched for the quiet participant who had not yet been invited to speak. A handwritten label read digest, but no one could say who had written it there. The term establish appeared in the revised file; Qin Mo checked its meaning against the original page. Shen Lan noticed the word fantasy in a note that someone had added after the first inspection.

**繁中 04**

他第一次進入訊問室，沒有抬頭尋找任何人頭上的字。計畫改了三次，每次修改都留下痕跡。秦墨認為這些修正比看似完美的初稿更有價值。就算結果不理想，團隊仍能知道該修正哪一項假設。沈嵐也注意到那名一直沒有發言機會的人。手寫標籤寫著「理解；消化」，卻沒有人知道是誰留下的。修訂檔案出現「建立」一詞，秦墨拿原始頁面核對它的意思。沈嵐注意到初次檢查後有人在筆記裡加上「幻想」一詞。

**EN 05**

An official version reached the room before the practical work was finished. Qin Mo read it once, then compared it with what they had actually measured. The difference was not always malicious, but it could still hurt somebody. Shen Lan asked that the correction be written in language the affected people could understand. The station corridor sounded ordinary after midnight. Qin Mo found that the hardest part of investigating a powerful claim was giving an ordinary doubt enough time. One witness used the word imaginary; Shen Lan noted that a description was not a measurement. Shen Lan repeated the instruction to intend, then asked whether anyone had the right to refuse. The term mercy appeared in the revised file; Qin Mo checked its meaning against the original page.

**繁中 05**

實際工作還沒完成，官方版本就傳到現場。秦墨讀了一次，再比對真正測得的結果。差異未必都是惡意，卻仍可能傷害人。沈嵐要求把更正寫成受影響者看得懂的語言。午夜後警局走廊聲音平凡。秦墨發現調查強大宣稱時最難的是給平凡疑問足夠時間。一位證人用了「想像中的」一詞，沈嵐指出形容並不等於測量。沈嵐重述「打算」的指示，再問當事人是否有權拒絕。修訂檔案出現「憐憫；仁慈」一詞，秦墨拿原始頁面核對它的意思。

**EN 06**

A witness asked whether the team was taking sides. Qin Mo answered that refusing to invent a fact was not the same as refusing to care. What mattered was whether the procedure protected someone whose story was less convenient to tell. Shen Lan listened without interrupting and wrote down the question exactly as it had been asked. The term obedience appeared in the revised file; Qin Mo checked its meaning against the original page. Shen Lan noticed the word observation in a note that someone had added after the first inspection. A handwritten label read regarding, but no one could say who had written it there.

**繁中 06**

一名見證人問團隊是否偏袒。秦墨回答，不捏造事實不代表冷漠。真正重要的是程序能否保護那個故事不方便被說出口的人。沈嵐沒有打斷，逐字記下原問題。修訂檔案出現「服從」一詞，秦墨拿原始頁面核對它的意思。沈嵐注意到初次檢查後有人在筆記裡加上「觀察」一詞。手寫標籤寫著「關於」，卻沒有人知道是誰留下的。

**EN 07**

Even after the group agreed on the immediate step, another question remained. The answer would not arrive through a dramatic announcement. Someone would have to return to the place, compare the earlier notes, and accept the possibility that the next piece of evidence might spoil an attractive theory. Qin Mo prepared to do exactly that. The interview recorder captured words but not the years of rehearsal behind them. Shen Lan insisted on laboratory tests before a dramatic confession became a charge. A handwritten label read scold, but no one could say who had written it there. The summary called the situation stingy; Qin Mo asked which observation justified that description. Shen Lan noticed the word triumph in a note that someone had added after the first inspection.

**繁中 07**

即使大家同意眼前步驟，另一個問題仍在。答案不會靠戲劇性公告降臨；有人得返回現場、比對舊筆記，並接受新證據可能推翻漂亮理論。秦墨準備做的正是這件事。偵訊錄音留下話語，留不下背後多年的排練。沈嵐堅持在戲劇性認罪變成指控前先完成實驗室檢驗。手寫標籤寫著「責罵」，卻沒有人知道是誰留下的。摘要以「小氣的」形容情況，秦墨追問這種說法有何觀察根據。沈嵐注意到初次檢查後有人在筆記裡加上「勝利」一詞。

### 理解測驗

**Q1. Which development is established in Chapter 24?**

A. The investigators decide that every earlier warning was fictional.

B. The producer was charged on ordinary evidence. Qin returned to the studio after investigators removed its screens and speakers.

C. The central problem disappears without further action.

**答案：B。** 製作人因一般證據遭起訴，秦墨在調查人員移走螢幕與喇叭後重返攝影棚。

**Q2. What new complication or decision changes the investigation in this chapter?**

A. All the witnesses agree to destroy their records.

B. The team abandons the people affected and declares a perfect victory.

C. For the first time he entered an interview room without searching the air above anyone's head.

**答案：C。** 他第一次進入訊問室，沒有抬頭尋找任何人頭上的字。


## CHAPTER 25｜The Sentence He Chose to Say｜他親口選擇的那句話

### 正文與翻譯

**EN 01**

Qin testified at the driver's hearing, describing precisely how he had mistaken a rehearsed statement for reliable testimony years earlier. Something had changed, but not everybody noticed it in the same order. Qin Mo listened to the account of the nearest worker, then to someone standing farther away. Their differences were not necessarily lies. Each person had been given a different view and a different reason to be afraid of admitting uncertainty. The interview recorder captured words but not the years of rehearsal behind them. Shen Lan insisted on laboratory tests before a dramatic confession became a charge. Shen Lan noticed the word bankrupt in a note that someone had added after the first inspection. A handwritten label read dew, but no one could say who had written it there.

**繁中 01**

秦墨在司機覆審中作證，清楚說明多年前如何誤把排練說法當成可靠供詞。某件事改變了，但人們發現它的順序不同。秦墨先聽近處工作人員的描述，再聽遠處旁觀者說法。差異不一定是謊言：每個人看到的角度不同，承認不確定時所要承擔的風險也不同。偵訊錄音留下話語，留不下背後多年的排練。沈嵐堅持在戲劇性認罪變成指控前先完成實驗室檢驗。沈嵐注意到初次檢查後有人在筆記裡加上「破產」一詞。手寫標籤寫著「露水」，卻沒有人知道是誰留下的。

**EN 02**

For the first time he entered an interview room without searching the air above anyone's head. Qin Mo refused to pretend the previous decision had solved everything. The practical result was still there, useful but narrow. For each thing the team knew, there were two things it had not measured. Qin Mo drew a line through the claims that depended on speculation and asked Shen Lan to keep the original record intact. The term disability appeared in the revised file; Qin Mo checked its meaning against the original page. Shen Lan noticed the word empire in a note that someone had added after the first inspection. Shen Lan repeated the instruction to endanger, then asked whether anyone had the right to refuse.

**繁中 02**

他第一次進入訊問室，沒有抬頭尋找任何人頭上的字。秦墨不願假裝前一次決定已解決所有問題。成果確實存在，卻有明確界限。每個已知事項旁都還有未測量的部分。秦墨劃掉靠猜測成立的說法，請沈嵐保留原始紀錄。修訂檔案出現「身心障礙」一詞，秦墨拿原始頁面核對它的意思。沈嵐注意到初次檢查後有人在筆記裡加上「帝國」一詞。沈嵐重述「危及」的指示，再問當事人是否有權拒絕。

**EN 03**

A small disagreement changed the schedule. Qin Mo wanted to proceed before evidence disappeared; Shen Lan wanted another check because a wrong conclusion might hurt people who could not easily defend themselves. They did not settle the matter by choosing a leader. They wrote down what a short, reversible test could actually establish. An official memo described the plan as enormous, but Qin Mo refused to treat an adjective as evidence. The term graph appeared in the revised file; Qin Mo checked its meaning against the original page. Shen Lan noticed the word haste in a note that someone had added after the first inspection.

**繁中 03**

一場小爭執改變了時程。秦墨想趁證據消失前行動；沈嵐要求再核對一次，因為錯誤結論可能傷害難以自辯的人。他們沒有靠選領袖解決爭議，而是列出短暫、可逆測試真正能證明什麼。官方備忘錄形容計畫「龐大的」，秦墨不願把形容詞當作證據。修訂檔案出現「圖表」一詞，秦墨拿原始頁面核對它的意思。沈嵐注意到初次檢查後有人在筆記裡加上「倉促」一詞。

**EN 04**

The court ordered a new review; Qin's final sentence was not a prediction but an apology he chose to speak aloud. The plan had been amended three times, and each change left a trace. Qin Mo valued those corrections more than a perfect-looking first draft. If the outcome proved disappointing, the group would still be able to identify which assumption needed repair. Shen Lan watched for the quiet participant who had not yet been invited to speak. Shen Lan noticed the word impression in a note that someone had added after the first inspection. A handwritten label read including, but no one could say who had written it there. The term instinct appeared in the revised file; Qin Mo checked its meaning against the original page.

**繁中 04**

法院命令重新覆審；秦墨最後說出的不是預言，而是自己決定出口的道歉。計畫改了三次，每次修改都留下痕跡。秦墨認為這些修正比看似完美的初稿更有價值。就算結果不理想，團隊仍能知道該修正哪一項假設。沈嵐也注意到那名一直沒有發言機會的人。沈嵐注意到初次檢查後有人在筆記裡加上「印象」一詞。手寫標籤寫著「包括」，卻沒有人知道是誰留下的。修訂檔案出現「本能」一詞，秦墨拿原始頁面核對它的意思。

**EN 05**

An official version reached the room before the practical work was finished. Qin Mo read it once, then compared it with what they had actually measured. The difference was not always malicious, but it could still hurt somebody. Shen Lan asked that the correction be written in language the affected people could understand. The interview recorder captured words but not the years of rehearsal behind them. Shen Lan insisted on laboratory tests before a dramatic confession became a charge. The report claimed insurance, but Qin Mo wanted an example instead of a reassuring noun. Shen Lan wrote intention in the margin and asked how the team would recognize it in practice. Shen Lan repeated the instruction to lengthen, then asked whether anyone had the right to refuse.

**繁中 05**

實際工作還沒完成，官方版本就傳到現場。秦墨讀了一次，再比對真正測得的結果。差異未必都是惡意，卻仍可能傷害人。沈嵐要求把更正寫成受影響者看得懂的語言。偵訊錄音留下話語，留不下背後多年的排練。沈嵐堅持在戲劇性認罪變成指控前先完成實驗室檢驗。報告聲稱有「保險」，秦墨要求具體例子，而不是安撫人的名詞。沈嵐在頁邊寫下「用意」，追問團隊實際上如何辨認。沈嵐重述「延長」的指示，再問當事人是否有權拒絕。

**EN 06**

A witness asked whether the team was taking sides. Qin Mo answered that refusing to invent a fact was not the same as refusing to care. What mattered was whether the procedure protected someone whose story was less convenient to tell. Shen Lan listened without interrupting and wrote down the question exactly as it had been asked. A handwritten label read liar, but no one could say who had written it there. The term paragraph appeared in the revised file; Qin Mo checked its meaning against the original page. Shen Lan noticed the word prompt in a note that someone had added after the first inspection.

**繁中 06**

一名見證人問團隊是否偏袒。秦墨回答，不捏造事實不代表冷漠。真正重要的是程序能否保護那個故事不方便被說出口的人。沈嵐沒有打斷，逐字記下原問題。手寫標籤寫著「騙子」，卻沒有人知道是誰留下的。修訂檔案出現「段落」一詞，秦墨拿原始頁面核對它的意思。沈嵐注意到初次檢查後有人在筆記裡加上「迅速的」一詞。

**EN 07**

After the hearing Qin Mo walked past the courthouse steps to meet an elderly witness who had traveled from a rural town. The woman carried a letter with no dramatic accusation, only dates and a signature that could still be verified after twenty years. For once Qin had no desire to search the vast air above her head for a final sentence. Her testimony had been voluntary; its meaning did not depend on a supernatural gift. Shen Lan joined them in the rain and offered the witness a lift to the station. Qin stayed behind long enough to read the letter once more. He could not rewrite the years the innocent driver had lost, and he could not make his apology a substitute for a new trial. The next case would begin with a question he was finally ready to ask: what can we actually prove?

**繁中 07**

聽證後，秦墨走下法院台階，去見一名從偏遠小鎮趕來的年長證人。婦人帶著一封信，沒有戲劇性指控，只有日期和二十年後仍能核對的簽名。秦墨第一次不想抬頭尋找她頭頂廣闊空氣裡的最後一句話。她自願作證，其意義不需要超自然能力來支撐。沈嵐在雨中走來，提議送證人到車站。秦墨留下來再讀一次信。他無法改寫無辜司機失去的年月，也不能把自己的道歉當成重新審判的替代。下一件案子開始時，他終於準備好先問：「我們究竟能證明什麼？」
